    إِنَّ رَسَولَ الله ? وَصَلَ إِلَى أُفُقٍ لَمْ يَعُدْ فِيهِ مَكَاناً لِجِبْرِيلَ، وَارْتَقَى لِمُسْتَوَى مِنَ النُّورِ وَدَرَجَةً مِنَ الْقُرْبِ وَالْحُضُورِ اِسْتَحَالَ مَعْهَا اِرْتَقَاءُ أَىِّ مَلَكٍ مُقَرَّبٍ أَوْ نَبِىٍّ مُرْسَلٍ إِلَى هَذَا الْاِرْتَقَاءِ:

    The Messenger of Allah ? reached towards a horizon where there never had been a place for Gabriel, and He rose to a level of light and a degree of nearness and presence, which was ever impossible for any closest Angel or any prophet messenger to rise towards this level of ascension:

    Qasida 28:26
    قَدِ اسْتَحَالَ ارْتِقَاءُ الْكُلِّ رَقْوَتِهِ *** فَدَنَا بِإِرْفَادِهِ يُرْوِي رَوَابِينَا
    Since the level of His ascension was impossible to reach by anyone…
    He came down out of His assistance, while He is watering our Heights

    فَكَانَ لِلْحَبِيبِ عَلَيْهِ أَفْضَلُ الصَّلَوَاتِ وَأَزْكَى التَّسْلِيمَاتِ الْمَكَانَةُ الزُلْفَى فحَقَّتْ لَهُ اَلْمُنَاجَاةُ وَالوَحْيُ وَالرُّؤْيَةُ دُونَ وَاسَطَةٍ أو حِجَابٍ، وَكَانَ هُوَ الْوَاسِطَةَ الْكُبْرَى وَالْحِجَابَ الْأَعْظَمَ، مَنْ بِهِ تَعرَّفَ الْخَلْقُ عَلَى الْحَقِّ، فَصَلِّ اللَّهُمَّ بِهِ مِنْهُ فِيْهِ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلِّمْ.

    So, the nearest place had always been for The beloved upon whom are the best prayers and the highest greetings, so he was certainly entitled of the intimate dialog, the revelation and the eye-witnessing directly without any kind of intermediary or any cover, while it was always He who had been the greatest intermediary and "The Greatest Cover", and by whom the creation was introduced to "The Divine Reality". O' Allah! Send prayers and peace greetings with Him, from Him, in Him, upon Him.

    لَمْ تَقِفْ تِلْكَ الْمَرتَبَةَ لِلْحَبِيبِ فَحَسْب .. وَلَكِنْ مِنْ كَرَمِ الْكِرَيمِ أَنْ جَعَلَهَا فِى خُلَفَاءِهِ وَوَرَثَتِهِ، وَيُشِيرُ إِلَيْهَا صَاحِبُ الْبَحْرِ الْمَدِيدِ فِى تَفْسِيرِ آيَةِ الرِّضَا مِنَ (الضُّحَى) فَيَقُولُ:

    And this rank has not been limited for "The Beloved" only. But it is out of the generosity of "The Generous" that He has transferred it into His successors and His inheritors. The author of "Al-Bahr Al-Madid" (saying from Imam Qusheiri by Sd. ibn Ajeeba) refers to it in the commentary of the Aya of satisfaction in Surah Ad-Duhaa (Surah Ad-Duhaa, 93:5) when he says:

    يُشيرُ إلى القَسَمِ بِضَحْوَةِ نَهارِ قَلْبِ الرسولِ ? عند اِنْتَشارِ شمسُ رُوحِهِ على بَشرِيَّتِهِ، وبِلَيلِ بَشريته عند أحكامِ الطبيعةِ، وسلوكِ آثارِ البشريةِ لِغَلَبَةِ سلطانِ الحقيقةِ (مَا وَدَّعَكَ رَبُّكَ) بِقَطْعِ فَيضِ النبوةِ والرسالةِ عن ظاهرِكَ (وَمَا قَلَى) بقَطْعِ فيضِ الولايةِ عن قَلْبِكَ (وَلَلْآخِرَةُ خَيْرٌ لَكَ مِنَ الْأُولَى) يعنى: أحوال نِهايَتِكَ أفضل وأكمل من أحوال بِدَايَتِكَ، لأنه ? لا يزال يطير بِجِنَاحَيْ الشريعةِ والطريقةِ فى جَوْ سماءِ الحقيقةِ ويترقَّى فى مقامات القربِ والكرامةِ.

    He refers to the oath by the morning of the day of heart of the Prophet ? with the sun of His soul scattering upon His humanity. And the oath by the night of His humanity with regulations of the human nature and the conduct and influence of the Humanity because of the dominating nature of the Sovereign of the Reality. "Your Lord had never abandoned you…" (Ad-Duhaa 93:3), by cutting the overflood of The Prophecy and The Messenger-hood from your appearance "…nor is He displeased" (Ad-Duhaa 93:3) by cutting the overflood of Sainthood from your heart "And the coming is better for you, than the present " (Ad-Duhaa 93:4) meaning: The conditions at your ending are much better and more perfect than the conditions at your beginning, because He ? is continuously and still flying by the wings of Sharia and The Tariqa in the atmosphere of the heaven of the reality and he is continuously and still rising in the stages of proximity and nobility.

    وسَرَى الْمُنَى فى الوجودِ فما كان له حد، فلم يَقْتَصِر على مرتَبة دون الأُخرَى، أو على اِمْرِئٍ دون غيرِهِ، وحسبُنَا فى ذلك طريقةِ أهل البيت ومسكِ الختامِ، فكان مُنى الرَعِيلُ الأول صُحبة الإمام فخر الدين رضى الله تعالى عنه، وَقَطَفُوا مِن دُرَرِ شرابِ وَصْلِ العارفينَ مِن بَين دَفَّتَى بَطائِن الأسرارِ حين تَبَدَّلَ عِندَها أَلِفٌ بِبَاهَا.

    And "The Desired Objective" travelled in the existence which did not have any limit or any end. So, it was never confined for any specific rank beneath another or any specific person beneath someone else. The Tariqa of "Ahlul-Bayt" (Family of the Prophet ?) and the "Misk-Al-Khitam" (The best ending) is sufficient for us. So, the desired objective of the first generation was to be in the company of Imam Fakhruddin (May Allah Almighty be pleased with him) and they plucked from the pearls of the drink of connection of the Gnostics from between the two sides of the interiors of the secrets at the time when the letter "Alif" was replaced in Qasaid with the letter "Baa".

    وإلى خامس الخلفاءِ الراشدين رضى الله عنه استمراراً لهذا الْمَنْهجُ الراقى في تعليم البشر، نراه يَهْمِسْ لنا بقوله: كونوا دُعاةً إلى الله وأنتم صَامِتُون، قالوا وكيف؟ قال: بأخلاقِكم، فما كان منه رضى الله عنه إلّا أن أوضح لنا حديث الحبيب المصطفى ? (الصَمْتُ حُكْمٌ، وَقَلِيلٌ فَاعِلْهُ) ورُوْىَ عن سيدنا لقمان الحكيم مِثْلَ هذا النَصْ، وعن أبى الدَرْدَاء كان يقول: تَعْلَّمُوا اَلصَمْتَ كما تَتعلمون الكلام، فإنَّ الصَّمْتَ حُكْمٌ عَظِيمٌ وكُنْ إلى أَنْ تَسْمَعَ أَحْرَصَ مِنْكَ إلى أن تتكلم، ولا تتكلم في شيء لا يَعْنِيكَ، ولا تَكُنْ مِضْحَاكاً مِنْ غَيرِ عَجَبٍ، ولا مَشَّاءً إلى غيرِ إِربٍ يعنى إلى غيرِ حاجةْ.
    And while moving on to the fifth of the Rightly Guided Caliphs (May Allah be satisfied with him) as a continuation for this superior curriculum in human education while we see him whispering to us: Become callers to Allah without speaking a word, and they asked and how is that possible? He replied: By your good character. And He (May Allah be satisfied with him) was only explaining for us, the meaning of the hadeeth of the beloved Mustafa ?: "The silence is a decision, and only few make it" and also Sayyidina Luqman Al-Hakim has narrated a similar narration. and Abi Dardaa used to say: Learn to be silent just as you learn to speak, because the silence is a great decision. Let the listening be more desirable to you than the talking. And do not speak in anything that does not concern you, and don't be funny without actual amazement, and do not wander around without requirement meaning without any actual need.

    فَعَلَى الْأَحْبَابِ التَرَفُّعُ بِأَخْلَاقِهِمْ ويُعِينُهُم عَلَى هَذَا كَثْرَةُ الْاِجْتِهَادِ فِي اَلذِّكْرِ وَالْعَبَادَةِ ومَحَبَّتِهِم لِإِخْوَانِهِمْ، لِتَكُونَ سَبَباً فِى نِجَاتِهمِ إِنْ شَاءَ الله.
    So, it is upon those beloved to enhance their characters and they are supported in this by a lot of diligence in doing "Dhikr", in worshipping and in loving their brothers, to become the reason for their salvation, May Allah be willing.

    وتَنْهَمِرُ حَبَّاتُ مَطَرِ لُؤْلُؤُ الْمُنَى لِتَعُمَّ سَائِرُ الْمُسْلِمِينْ وَيَتَجَلَّى نَوَالُ مُنَاهُم فِى رِضَاءِ الْوَالِدَينِ، وَفِى الْزَوجَةِ الصَالِحَةَ، وَيَمْتَدُّ حَتَّى يَصِلْ إِلى تَربيةِ الْأَطفالِ، ويَتَفَرَّعُ لِيَتَواصلَ وَيتجّلى فِى نعمةِ العلمِ، لِيَفِيضَ بعدَها على عمومِ معاملاتِ الْمُسلمِ مع سائرِ الخلقِ، وَدُرَرُ هَذَا الموطنِ فى رِضَاءِ الْبَارِى بِالْقَبولِ وَالْعِرفانِ مِنَ الْحنانِ المنانِ.
    And the raindrops of the pearls from the desired objective are pouring down, to spread widely prevailing over all of the Muslims, and the great giving of their desired objective appears in the satisfaction of the parents, and in the righteous wife, and extends until it reaches the cultivation of the children, and it divides while keep progressing and then manifests as the blessing of the knowledge, then afterwards it overflows upon all dealings of the Muslim with the other creation. And the pearls of this place are in the satisfaction of "Al-Bari" (The Creator) by acceptance and appreciation from "Al-Hannan" (The Affectionate), "Al-Mannan" (The Benefactor).

    ربمّا جاء يومً نجلِسَ فيه معاً، لا لِنَختلف أو نَأْتَلِف أو حتى لِنَتْعَاتَبْ أو نَتَصَافَحْ، وإنما لِنكشِفَ كم غرَّتْنا الدنيا وجَعلَتْنا نَتَذَكَّر حقُوقَنَا وننسَى واجِباتِنا، ونجد أنَّ ما حَدَثَ بالأمسِ القريبِ كان عن سوءِ فهمٍ، فجعلتْ الأخَّ يأخُذُ موقفاً من أخيهِ، وحينَها يَتَّضِحُ أنه لم تكن هناك مشكلةً منَ الْأساسِ، بَلْ تَصَرُّفَاتٌ حَدَثَتْ بَيْنَهُمْ دُونَ مُبَرِّرْ وَكُلٌ آخِذٌ جَانِباً منَ الآخَر.

    Perhaps the time has come for us to sit together, not for the purpose that we argue or that we become united or to blame each other, or even to shake hands, but only for the purpose so that we can discover how much the worldly life has deluded us and made us focus upon our rights while we have forgotten our duties, and we for sure discover, that what has happened in the near past was a result of a misunderstanding, which had made a brother turn aside (hold a grudge 59:10) from his brother, and then it turns out that there basically was no problem at all, but rather some actions had taken place between them without any justified reason and each one had turned aside from the other.

    ففى كُلِّ طَوْرٍ من الأطوارِ تجدُ فروعُ الْمُنَى وَدُرَرِهِ تَحِنُّ إلى أَصْلِ الْعَهْدِ (بِدَايَةُ الطَّرِيقُ) الذِّي اِرْتَوَى بِالسِّرِّ الِّذِي سَرَى فِيْنَا (كمال الدين).

    In every generation from among the generations(Khalifa) (71:14) you will discover branches (24:36) of the desired objective ? and the abundance of the pearls (Kaukab,) from him, which are craving for the origin of the Covenant (the beginning of the Tariqa), which are watered (Water of the Prophet ?) by the secret, which is circulating inside us (Kamal al-Din (Perfecter of the religion), Maulana Shaikh Ibrahim (ra)).

    ويقول المولى سبحانه (وَالَّذِينَ هُمْ لِأَمَانَاتِهِمْ وَعَهْدِهِمْ رَاعُونَ (8) وَالَّذِينَ هُمْ عَلَى صَلَوَاتِهِمْ يُحَافِظُونَ (9) أُولَئِكَ هُمُ الْوَارِثُونَ (10) المؤمنون، مَدَحَ المولى سبحانه (الخلفاء) أَصحابُ اَلْأَمَانَاتِ (رُعَاةُ الرَّعِيَّةِ) بِأنهم مُراعِينَ لعهد الله اَلْمُتَمَثِّلْ فِى السِّرْ اَلذي سَرَى فِيهِم مِنْ السِّر النَّبَوِى، أَلَا وَهُوَ هِدَايَةُ الضَّالِينَ، وَرَدُّ الشَّارِدِينَ، ثُم أَفْصَحَ فى خِتَامِ الْآيَةَ بِأَنَّهُم (الوَارِثُونَ) فَهَلَّا وَعَيْتُم مَنْ هُمْ (اَلْخُلَفَاءُ الرَّاشِدِينَ) عَلَى اخْتِلَافِ الْمُسَمَّيَاتِ التى أَطْلَقَهَا عَلَيْهِم اَلْمُولَى الكريم وَحَبِيبَهُ وَمُصَطَفَاهُ.

    The Lord Almighty have said: "And those who are watchfully guarding upon their trusts and their pledge - And those who, continuously, are maintaining their prayers - They are indeed those who are the inheritors" (Al-Mominun 23:8 - 10). The Lord Almighty praises the "The Khalifas" the owners of the trusts, "Guarding Watchers over the flocks", because they are considerate of the Covenant of Allah. Represented by the secret which is circulating inside them coming from the Prophetic Secret. Unquestionably, the secret is in the guidance of those deviated and in the return of the runaways. Then in the end of the Aya, it is made clear that they are indeed "The Inheritors". So, shouldn't you be aware of the identity of these "Rightly Guided Khalifas"? despite of the different denominations given to them by the Lord Almighty and His "Beloved" and His "Chosen".

    يقول المصطفى ? ?سَوُّوْا صُفُوفَكُمْ، فَإِنَّ تَسْوِيَةَ الصُّفُوفِ مِنْ تَمَامِ الصَّلَاةِ ? إن المسلمون فى مشارق الأرضِ ومغارِبِها يُؤَدُّونَ الصلواتِ الْخَمْسْ ويُحَافظون على تَسْوية الصفْ، ونَسُوا أو تَنَاسَوْا أَنْ يَسَوُّوْا صُفُوفَ وِحْدَتِهم بين الْأُمَمْ، فَوِحْدَةُ صَفِّ الْعَالَمْ الإسْلَامِى أَهَمُّ رَكِيزَةٍ مِنْ رَكَائِزِ التَّقَدُّمِ، فَعِندما تَهَاوَنَ الْمُسْلِمُ فى وِحْدَةِ الصَّفِّ، فَقَد اِسْتَهَانَ بِكرامةِ وَطَنِهِ، فَإِقامةُ كرامةِ الْوَطَنِ مِنَ الْإِيمَانِ.

    Al-Mustafa ? says: "Allign your rows, because the allignment of the rows is from the perfection of the prayer". The Muslims in the East and the West of the Earth perform the five prayers and watchful regarding the alignment of the row while they have forgotten or neglects to align the rows of their unity among the nations. Because the unity of the Islamic world is the most important pillar of the progression, but when the Muslim neglects the unity of the row then he has already disgraced the dignity and sanctity of his origin, because the dignity of the origin is from The Faith.

    ففى صَدْرِ الْإِسلامِ عندما كانتْ الصحابةُ وِحدةً وَاحدةً حَوْل الرسول ? لا يَلْتَفُّونَ حَوْلَ غَيْرِه، يُرَاقِبُونَهُ وَلَا يُرَاقِبُونَ غَيْرَهْ، كَانَ لَهُم الْفَتْحُ الْأَكْبَرُ وَالنَصْرُ الْمُبِينْ، فَكَانُوا لَا يَرَونَ غَيرَ الرسولِ ? رَغْمَ حُبِّهِم لِلصَّدِيقِ، ورَغْم حُبِّ رسولِ الله ? لَهُ، فَلَمْ يَجْعَلُوا يوماً نِدّاً لِرسولِ لله ? حتى اَلصّديقَ نَفْسَهْ.

    So, In the initial period of Islam when the companions were one unit around the prophet ?. They did not circulate around any other than him. They observed him and did not observe any other than him, so they obtained the greatest opening (Conquest) and the obvious support (Victory). They did not used to look at any other than the Prophet ? despite their love for "The Siddiq", and despite the love of the Messenger of Allah ? towards him. They never made, on any given day, an analogue comparable for The Messenger of Allah ?, not even to "The Siddiq" himself.

    وعِنْدَما آلَتِ الْخِلَافةُ إِلَى الصَدِّيقِ كَانَ رَضِىَ الله تَعَالَى عَنْهُ بِمَنْزِلَةِ رَسُولِ الله ? وَسَطَ قَوْمِهِ، مِصْدَاقاً لِقَوْلِهِ ?اَلشَّيْخُ فِى قَوْمِهِ كَالنَّبِىِّ فِى أُمَّتِهِ? وَكَانَ لَقَبُهُ (خَلِيفَةُ رَسُولِ الله) وَكَانَ سَائِرُ اِلصَّحَابَةُ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الصِدِّيقِ عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ الله ? فَلَا يَرَوْنَ مَعْ (اَلْخَليفَةِ) لِرسُولِ الله أَحَداً غَيرُهْ، لِذَلِكَ اِتَّحَدَتْ اَلْأُمَّةُ وَاشْتَدَّ عَضَدِهَا، وَحِينَ اخْتَلَفُوا وَكَثُرَتْ الْآرَاءُ، وَتَعَدَّدَتِ الْمَفَاهِيمُ حَولَ اَلْخِلَافَةِ، نَشَبَتْ اَلْحُرُوبُ، بَلْ وَنَالَ اَلْقَاصِى وَالْدَانِى مِنْهَا.

    When the Khilafa (Succession) was transferred to "The Siddiq", he was (May Allah be pleased with him) upon the position of the Messenger of Allah ? in midst of His people, approving His ? saying "The Shaikh among his people is as a prophet among his nation". And his title was "The successor of the messenger of Allah" and the rest of the companions were in the position of "The Siddiq", as He was during the pledge (time) of the Messenger of Allah ?, so they never considered anyone to be with the Khalifa of the messenger of Allah. As a result of this, the nation was united and became intense in power. But when they differed and the opinions increased into many and the understandings surrounding the Khilafa turned into numerous, then the internal wars broke out and those from the far and wide succeeded in disgracing the nation.

    ولَمَّا كان الشيطانُ يَدْخُلُ دَائماً فى كل أَمْرٍ مِن أُمورِ الدِّينِ الْقويمِ لِيُحيِّد النَّاسَ عَنِ الطَرِيقِ الْمُسْتَقِيمِ، جَاءَ الْبَعْضُ وَاتّخَذَ مِن مُسَمَّى الخِلَافَةِ أَوْ اَلْإمَارَةِ سَيفاً يَتَسَلَّطُوا بِهِ عَلَى رِّقَابِ الْعِبَادِ، فَاسْتَبَاحُوْا بِهِ دِمَاءَ الْبَشَرِ التى حَرَمَتْهَا اَلشَّرَائِعُ السَّمَاوِيَّةْ وَالقَوانِينَ الْإنسانِيَّةْ، حُجَّتُهُم فِى ذَلِكَ اَلدَّعْوَةُ إلى الْإسْلَامِ، فَاجْتَزَأُوْا النُصُوصَ تَاَرةً، وَتَارَةً أُخْرَى فَسَّرُوْهَا بِأَهْوَائِهِمْ، وَنَصَّبُوْا أَنْفُسَهُمْ أَوْصِيَاءَ عَلَى النَّاسِ وَعَلَى الْمُجْتَمِعَاتِ، وَأَتَوْا بِفِكْرٍ عَقِيمٍ، وَضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ، وَأَصَّلُوا لِفِكْرَةِ اَلتَّطَرُّفِ أَينمَا حَلُّوا، وَحَسِبُوا أَنَّهُم يُحْسِنُونَ صَنِعاً، وَهُمُ الْخَاسِرِينَ أَعْمَالاً، وَمَا كَانَتْ اَلْخِلَافَةُ يَوْماً عَلَى هَذَا الْحَالِ، وَإِنَّمَا هِىَ رَمْزٌ لِلتَّوْحِيدِ وَهَدَفُ لِاسْتِكْمَالِ مَسِيرَةُ الْحِياةِ، فَتَوْحِيدُ الْكَلِمَةِ وَتَوْحِيدُ الْجُهُودِ، يَسْعَى بِنَا لِبِنَاءِ مُجْتَمَعِ قَوِىِّ مُتَمَاسِكْ، لَا تُؤَثِّرْ فِيهِ التَّيَارَاتُ الْخَبِيثَةُ وَالْأفْكَارُ اَلْبَائِسَةُ التى تَسْعَى لْخَرابِ الْمُجْتَمَعِ بَلْ وَخَرابِ الْإِنْسَانِ.

    Since Satan always have entered into every matter of matters of the upright religion to deviate the people away from the balanced right path. Some came and took the name of the Khilafa or the Emirate as a sword to be imposed upon the necks of the worshippers, and they legitimized by it, the spilling of blood of the human, which had been forbidden by every divine codex (Sharia's) and the laws of humanity. Their argument in doing that, is the invitation to Islam. So, sometimes they would cut the divine text into pieces taking it out of context, and at other times they would interpret it according to their whims (inclination and impulses), and instated themselves as supervisors above the people and the communities, and they brought an infertile concept and an evident aberration, they established the foundation of the concept of extremism wherever they are, while they figured that they are accomplishing an excellent work, while they actually are those who are the losers of all their deeds (43:36, 5:5), and the Khilafa had never been like this, on any given day, but it is a symbol for the unification and a target to complete the march of life. While uniting the word and uniting the efforts, it pursues us to build a strong and cohesive society, which is not affected by the pernicious tendencies and wretched ideologies that seeks to ruin the society and rather the devastation of man.

    وَالْكِتَابُ الْكَرِيمِ ضَرَبَ لَنَا أَعْظَمَ وَأَرْوَعَ اَلْأَمْثِلَةِ اَلْمُتَمَثِّلَةْ فِى سَيْدِنَا هارون عليه السلام، حينَ اسْتَخْلَفَهُ كَلِيمُ الله على قَومِهِ، وَعَبَدَ بَنُو إِسرائيلَ اَلْعِجْلَ، وَلَمَّا رَجَعَ اَلْكَلِيمُ وَعَاتَبَ سَيَّدَنَا هَارُون، فَقَالَ لَهُ كَلِمَةً سَجَّلَهَا التَّارِيخُ لِتَكونَ رَمْزاً على مَرِّ الْعُصُورِ، وَتَتَفَاوَتْ عَليها اَلدَّهُورْ (خَشِيتُ أَنْ تَقُولَ فَرَّقْتَ بَيْنَ بَنِى إِسْرَائِيلَ) 94 طه، أَنْ جَعَلْتُهُم فِرْقَتَينِ، وَيَالَهَا مِنْ سُخْرِيَّةْ، لَمْ تَتَفَرَّقْ اَلْأُمَّةْ إلى فِرْقَتَينْ بَلْ صَارَتْ فِرَقٌ بِينَ هَؤلُاءِ وَهَؤُلَاءِ، وَمِن شِدَّةِ الْحَمَاقَةِ، تَجِدُ لِكُلِّ قَوْمٍ مِنْهُمْ حُجَّةً يَسْتَنِدُ إِلَيْهَا لِيُبَرِّرَ بِهَا مُخَالَفَتِهِم ??? ورسولهِ وَأُولِى الْأَمْرِ، وَلمْ يَؤْوِى أَحَدُهُمْ إلى رُكْنٍ شَدِيدٍ أَوْ إِلَى رَأْىٍ سَدِيدٍ.

    The Noble Book put forth for us the greatest and most magnificent examples represented by our Master Harun (as), when he was appointed as a successor by the speaker of Allah over his people, and then sons of Israel started to worship the calf. When the Speaker returned and blamed our Master Harun, he replied to him with a word, which is recorded by history to become a great symbol throughout all ages passing on though time. "I feared that you would say: You caused division between the sons of Israel" (Taha 20:94), that you have divided them into two fractions. And what an irony, that the Islamic nation didn't just divide into two divisions, but instead became many sects between these and those. By the severity of foolishness, you will find each sect among them having an argument, based upon justifying their opposition of Allah and His Messenger and the "Those of Authority", and not one of them relied upon any solid proof or any sound opinion.

    فَالْأُمَّةُ الْإِسْلَامِيَّةْ لَنْ تَعُودَ إِلَى عَصْرِهَا اَلذَّهَبِى حَتَّى تَفِيقَ مِنْ غَفْلَتِهَا. وَيَجْتَمِعُوا عَلَى كِتَابِ الله وَسُنَّةِ رَسُولِهِ ?.

    Therefore, the Islamic nation will never return to its golden age until it wakes up from its heedlessness. And they unite themselves upon the Holy Book of Allah and the Sunnah of the Messenger of Allah ?.

    فَوِحْدَةُ صَفِّ الْأُمَّةُ الْإِسْلَامِيَّةُ عَلَّمَهَا رَسُول اللهِ ? لِلْبَشَرِيةِ، لِصَغَارِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ قَبْلَ كِبَارِهِم، عَلَّمَهَا لَهُم فِى رُكْنِ الصَّلَاةِ فَقَالَ ? ?مُرُوْا أَوْلَادَكُمْ بِالصَّلَاةِ لِسَبْعٍ، وَاضْرِبُوهُمْ عَلَيْهَا لِعَشْرٍ? عَلِّمُوهُم أَنْ يَكُونُوا خَلْفَ إِمَامٍ وَاحِدٍ وَلَا يَتَفَرَّقُوا شِيعاً، فَهَلْ رَأَيْتُمْ جَمَاعَةً بِإِمَامَينِ؟!!

    The Messenger of Allah ? taught the unity of the ranks of the Islamic Nation to the humanity, He taught it to the young among the Muslims before to their elders. He taught them in regarding the pillar of the prayer while He ? said: "Ask your children, from the age of seven, to pray, and reprimand them upon it, on the age of ten." Teach them, that they must be united behind only one imam and never disperse into sects. Have you perhaps ever seen a congregational prayer lead by two imams?!!

    فَإِذَا نَظَرْنَا إِلَى أَعْدَاءِ الْإِسْلَامِ نَجِدَهُمْ يَدْعُونَ إِلَى صِّفَاتِ الْمُسْلِمِ غَيرَ أَنَّهُمْ لِيْسُوْا مُسْلِمِينَ، فَتَجِدَهُمْ يُشِيرُونَ إِلَى تَوْحِيدِ صُفُوْفِهِمْ، وَأَنْ تَكُونَ كَلِمَتُهُمْ وَاحِدَةً، وَاحْتِرَامُ رُمُوزُهُمْ، وَالرَّحْمَةُ بِصَغِيرِهِمْ وَالْمُحَبَّةُ شَعَارُهُمْ وَحُسْنُ الْمُعَامَلَةُ دَيْدَنَهُمْ.

    When we look at the enemies of Islam, we find them calling to the qualities of the Muslim, while they are not Muslims. You find them pointing towards uniting their ranks and that their word becomes one. Respecting their symbols, compassion for their young, love is their slogan, and good treatment is their nature.

    وَعِنْدَمَا حَلَّ اَلْمُصْطَفَى ? بِالْمَدِينَةِ الْمُنَوَّرَةِ أَرْسَى فَيِهَا تَعَالِيمَ الْإِسْلَامِ اَلسَّمِحَةْ، فَأَمَرَ بِبَنَاءِ اَلْمَسْجِدَ لِيَجْمَعَ فِيْهِ اَلْجَمْعَ اَلْكَرِيمَ مِنَ اَلصَّحَابَةِ وَمَنْ بَعْدُهِمْ مِنَ التَّابِعِينَ.

    When Al-Mustafa ? came to stay in Medina, he laid down the teachings of the largehearted Islam, So He ordered building of the mosque to collect the noble assembly of the companions and also for who were follow after them.

    وَلِكَيْ لَاْ يَكُونَ هُنَاكَ فِرْقَتَيْنِ عَلَّمَهُمْ اَلْمُعَلِّمُ الْأَوَّلْ مَعْنَى اَلْإِخَاءِ، فَآخَى بَينَ الْمُهَاجِرِينَ وَالْأَنْصَارِ لِيَكُونُوا صَفَّاً وَاحِداً.

    So, that they should not become two fractions. That's why, The First Teacher ? taught them the meaning of the brotherhood. By creating a brotherhood between the immigrants and the supporters so to become one united rank.

    ثُمَّ عَلَّمَهُمْ رَسُولُ الْبَشَرِيَّةْ اَلْعَفْوَ فِى أَبْهَى صُوَرِهِ عِنْدَمَا قَامَ بِأَعْدَادِ (وَثِيقَةُ الْمَدِيْنَةْ) أَوْ كَمَا أَطْلِقَ عَلَيْهَا اَلْبَعْضُ (دُسْتُوْرُ الْمَدِيْنَةِ) وَهِيَ اَلَّتِي تُنَظِّمُ عَلَاقَةُ جَمِيعُ الْمُوَاطِنِينَ فِى الْمَدِينَةِ عَلَى اِخْتَلَافِ دِيَانَاتِهِمْ، وَلَمْ يَتَعَصَّبْ رَسُولِ الْإِسْلَامِ لِلْإِسْلَام ِبَلْ نَظَّمَ عَلَاقَاتِ التَّعَامُلِ بَينَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ وَالْيَهُودِ عَلَى اِخْتَلَافِ طَوَائِفِهِمْ، ثُمَّ أَسَّسَ سُوقٌ تِجَارِى حَتَّى يَكْسِرُ اَلْاِحْتِكَارَ، بَلْ وَنَهَى عَنْ اِحْتَكَارِ مَعَايْشِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ.

    The messenger of humanity then taught them to pardon in the most beautiful manner, when he prepared (The Bill of Medina) or as some have called it (The constitution of Medina), which regulates the relationship of all citizens in the city despite of their different religions. The messenger of Islam was not discriminative in favor of Islam, but rather He organized a system of dealings between the Muslims and the Jews despite of the differences of their religions, and then He founded a commercial market to break the monopoly and even forbad the monopoly of the Muslim livelihoods.

    فَأَصْبَحَتْ اَلدَّوْلَةُ الْإِسْلَامِيَّةُ دُوْلُةً عُظْمَى ذَاتَ كِيَانٍ وَتَجَلَّتْ قُوَّتَهَا فِى اِتِّحَادِ كَلِمَتِهَا وَالسَّيْرُ خَلْفَ رَسُولِهَا اَلْأَكْرَمْ ?.

    The Islamic State became a great state with a prevailing existence and its power was manifested in the unity upon its word and the following behind its Noble Messenger ?.

    نِلْنَا مُنَانَا وَمَا فَوْقَ الْمُنَى دُرَراً
    We have obtained our desired objective…
    and that which is above the desired objective, as an abundance of pearls
    (Qasida 28:13)
    ***

    Qasida 28:21
    عَادَ الْإِخَاءُ كَأَنْ لَمْ يُفْنِهِ زَمَنٌ *** وَأُشْهِدَ الْكَوْنُ آياً مِنْ تَآخِينَا
    The brotherhood had returned like though time had never affected it…
    And the universe witnessed signs of proofs from our fraternity

    Surah Fath (48:20)
    وَعَدَكُمُ اللَّهُ مَغَانِمَ كَثِيرَةً تَأْخُذُونَهَا فَعَجَّلَ لَكُمْ هَ?ذِهِ وَكَفَّ أَيْدِيَ النَّاسِ عَنكُمْ وَلِتَكُونَ آيَةً لِّلْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَيَهْدِيَكُمْ صِرَاطًا مُّسْتَقِيمًا
    Allah has promised you much booty that you will take [in the future] and has hastened for you this [victory] and withheld the hands of people from you - that it becomes be a sign for the believers and [that] He may guide you to a straight path.

    تَحَدَّثْنَا سَابِقاً عَنْ أَخْطَارِ الْعَولَمَةْ فَمَازالَ هُنَاكَ طَائِفَةً تَنْشُرُ مَا يَحْلُوْ لَهَا بِاِسْمِنَا وَرَسْمِنَا دُونَ اَلرَّجُوعَ لِشَخْصِنَا أَوْ اَلرَّجُوعَ لِلْقَنَوَاتِ اَلشَّرَعِيَّةِ اَلْمُتَّصِلَةِ بِنَا، فَيَتَحَدَّثُوا بِاِسْمِ الطَّرِيقَةْ دُونَ إِذْنِ صَاحِبِهَا، وَلَمْ يَسْتَنْكِفْ اَلْأَحْبَابُ نُصْحِي اَلَّذِي كَانَ لِوَجْهِ الله مَأْمُونِ الْجَنَابْ، فَمَتَى يَثُوبُوا لِأَنْفُسِهِم، وَيَتُوبُوا إِلَى الرَّبِّ الرَّحِيمْ، فَإِذَا كَانَ هَؤُلَاءِ اِرْتَكَبُوا أَخْطَاءً، فَادِحَةً فَالْخَطَأُ الْأَكْبَرُ مِنْ الَّذِينَ اِسْتَمَعُوا لَهُمْ وَتَجَاوَبُوْا مَعْهُمْ بَلْ وَتَبَادَلُوْا أَطْرَافَ الْحَدِيثِ فِيمَا بَيْنَهُمْ.

    We have previously spoken about the dangers of globalization (speech by Maulana Shaikh Ibrahim ra. in year 2000), but there is still a faction circulating what pleases themselves, by using our name and using our symbols, without directing it back for our approval or directing it back to the legitimate connecting channels appointed by us. So, they speak using the name of the Tariqa without the permission of its owner. And the lovers were never haughty about my advice, which is for the face of Allah, from the side of safety. So, when will they cure themselves, and repent to the merciful Lord? And, if some mistakes had been made by these, then the greatest mistakes were made by those, who had listened to them, had responded to them and even they had exchanged the conversations among each other.

    وَلاَ يَسْتَنْكِفُ الأَحْبَابُ نُصْحِي *** لِوَجْهِ اللَّهِ إِنِّي قَدْ نَصَحْتُ
    The lovers will never be haughty of my advice …
    And I have certainly, already given advice, for the face of Allah
    Qasida 41:1

    فَأَيْنَ الْعَاكِفُونَ عَلَى كَلاَمِي *** لِوَجْهِ اللَّهِ مَأْمُونِ الْجَنَابِ؟
    Where are those completely devoted to my saying …
    for the face of Allah, from the side of Safety
    Qasida 74:11

    وَهَذَا الْحَالُ يَنْطَبِقُ عَلَى حَالِ أَصْحَابِ حُبُّ الرِّئَاسَةِ اَلَّذِينَ دَعَوْا اَلسُّذَّجَ مِنْ أَبْنَاءِ الطَّرِيقَةِ لِأَنْفُسِهِمْ، أَوْ دَعَوْهُمْ لِلْاِلْتِفَافِ حَوْلَ غَيُرُنَا، وَفِى النَّهَايَةِ خَسِرُوْا أَنْفُسَهُمْ وَلْمْ يُصِيْبُوْا اَلطَّرِيقَ بِسِهَامِهِمْ وَأَفْكَارِهِمْ اَلضَّالَةْ، وَهَؤُلَاءِ أَوْ هَؤُلَاءِ كَأَنَّهُمْ فِى عِلْمِنَا قَدْ أَسْهَمُوْا.

    This case applies upon those who have the love of power, who are those who have drawn the naive fools of the Tariqa towards themselves, or drawn them to gather around others than us. But in the end, they had lost themselves and they never harmed the Tariqa by their assaults nor by their misconstrued ideas. And these or these, think, as if they have made some contribution to our knowledge.

    تَمْتَلِئُ اَلْحَيَاةُ بِالْمُسَرَّاتِ وَالْمُنَغِّصَاتِ، وَقَدْ تَتَكَاثَفُ الْأَحْزَانُ عَلَى الشَّخْصِ فَيَشْعُرُ بِضِيقِهَا وَكَدَرِهَا وَيُصْبِحُ كَارِهًا لَهَا، وَقَدْ تَجْتَمِعُ لَهُ اَلْحَيَاةُ بِكُلِّ الْمُسَرَّاتِ فَيَفْرَحُ بِهَا وَيَرَاهَا كَالْجَنَّةِ، وَمِنَ الْيَسِيرُ فَهْمُ تَغَيُّرِ الْإِنْسَانِ إِنْ فَهِمْنَا طَبِيعَةَ تَكْوِينَ النَّفْسِ الْبَشَرِيَّةِ، قَالَ الْحَقُّ تَعَالَى (إِنَّ الْإِنسَانَ خُلِقَ هَلُوعًا * إِذَا مَسَّهُ الشَّرُّ جَزُوعًا * وَإِذَا مَسَّهُ الْخَيْرُ مَنُوعًا * إِلَّا الْمُصَلِّينَ * الَّذِينَ هُمْ عَلَى? صَلَاِتِهِمْ دَائِمُونَ) المعارج.

    Life is filled with delights and troubles, and the sorrows may intensify upon the person, so that he feels its tightness and its distress and becomes averse towards it, or the life may collect all delights for him, so that he rejoices by it and he sees it as a paradise. It would be easy to understand the change of the mankind, if we were to understand the nature of the formation of the human soul. The True Almighty have said: "Indeed, mankind was created impatient: - Anxious, when evil touches him - Niggardly, when good touches him - Except the observers of their prayers - Those who are constant in their prayer" (Al-Ma'arij 70:19 - 23).

    إِنَّ مَفْهُومَ تَكْوِينْ وَحْدَةِ الْإِنْسَانِ وَخَلْقَهُ وَدَرَجَةَ اِمْتِثَالِهِ لِشَرَائِع ِرَبِّهِ، لَأَمْرٌ شَغَلَ اَلْبَشَرُ مُنْذُ الْقِدَمِ، فَإِنْ أَرَدْنَا تَبْسِيطَ مَعْنَى الدِّينِ فَهُوَ عِبَارَةٌ عَنْ عَبْدٍ وَرَبٍّ وَصِلَةٌ بَيْنَهُمَا تُسَمَّى (اَلدِّينَ) وَفِى أَمْرِ خَلْقِ النَّفْسِ الْبَشَرِيَّةِ مِنَ التُّرَابِ وَالْمَاءِ لِخَلْقِ سَيَّدَنَا آدَمَ حَسَبُ الْأَوَامِرِ الْإِلهِيَّةِ مِنَ الْاِسْمِ (الْبَارِئ) ثُمَّ إِلَى (اَلْمُصَوِّرِ) ثُمَّ إِلَى (الْخَالِقِ) وَكَانَ آدَمُ مِنْ أَدِيمِ الْأَرْضِ فَسُمِّىَ آدَمَ، وَقَدْ خُلِقَ فِى الْعَرْشِ، وَفِى السِّيرَةِ عَنْ سَيْدَنَا عُمَرْ بِنْ اَلْخِطَابْ عَنْ اَلنَّبِى ? قَالَ (فَقَالَ الله: يَا آدَمَ، وَكَيفَ عَرَفْتَ مُحَمَّدًا وَلَمْ أَخْلُقُهُ؟ قَالَ: يَا رَبّْ لِأَنَّكَ لَمَّا خَلَقْتَنِي بِيَدِكَ وَنَفَخْتَ فِىَّ مِنْ رُوْحِكَ، رَفَعْتُ رَأْسِي فَرَأَيْتُ عَلَى قَوَائِمِ الْعَرْشِ مَكْتُوباً: لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا الله مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ الله) فَهُوَ مَخْلُوقٌ فِى الْعَرْشِ، وَهُوَ بَدَنِيّاً مَخْلُوقٌ مِنْ تُرَابٍ وَمَاءٍ، ثُمَّ وَضَعَ فِيهِ اَلْأَرْواحَ اَلثَلَاثَ:

    The concept of the composition of the unit of the human, and his creation and his ability of compliance with the laws of his Lord, is indeed a thing, which has occupied the human beings since ancient times. If we were to simplify the meaning of the religion, then it is a terminology based upon the relation between a slave and a Lord, which is named "The Religion". And in the idea of the creation of the human being from "Dust" and "Water" to the creation of our Master Adam according to the divine orders, starting from the divine name "Al-Bari" (The inventor) and then to "Al-Musawir" (The designer) and then to "Al-Khaliq" (The Creator) and since Adam was from the "Adeem" (solum) of the Earth, he was named Adam. And he was created in the throne. In the biography of the Prophet ?, Sayyidina Omar ibn al-Khattab narrated from the Prophet ? saying "Allah asked: O' Adam, how did you know Mohamed, while I have yet not created him? he replied: O' my Lord, because when you created me by your hand and blew into me from your soul: I raised my head and saw written upon the legs of the Throne:- There is no God but Allah Muhammad is the Messenger of Allah.". So, He is a created in the throne, and he is physically a creation from dust and water, and then there had been put inside him, the three souls:

    1. وَاحِدَةٌ مِنَ الرِّيحِ الصِّرْفِ وَتُسَمَّى بِالرُّوحِ (الْبَهِيمِى) وَوَظِيفَتُهَا تَدْبِيرُ وَظَائِفِ الْجَسَدِ الْلَاإِرَادِى، كَوَظَائِفِ الْقَلْبِ وَالْكُلَى وَمَا شَابَهَ.

    1) One is from The Pure Wind and is called the Spirit of "Al-Bahimi" (The Bestiality) and its purpose is to operate the functions of the autonomic body, such as the functions of the heart, kidneys and the likes.

    2. ثُمَّ اَلرُّوحُ الثَّانِيَّةُ وَهِيَ رُوحُ (التَّكْرِيمِ) وَهِيَ مِنَ نُّورِ رُوحِ الْقُدُسِ (وَلَقَدْ كَرَّمْنَا بَنِى آدَمَ) 70 الإسراء، أَيْ وَضَعْنَا فِيهِ رُوحُ التَّكْرِيمِ، وَهِيَ تَنْزِلُ فِى مَوَاطِنِ الْحَوَاسِ، كَوْنَهُ: سَمِيعاً، بَصِيراً، حَيّاً، مُرِيداً، قَادِراً، عَالِماً، وَمُتَكَلِّماً.

    2) Then the second spirit is the spirit of "At-Takreem" (The Honouring), and it is from the light of the divine spirit, "And We have certainly honoured the children of Adam" (Al-Isra, 17:70), meaning that we have put inside of him, the spirit of "At-Takreem" (The Honouring), and it descends into the domains of the senses, coming from the divine attributes: Hearing, Seeing, Alive, Desiring, Ability, Knowing, and Speaking.

    3. وَالرُّوحُ الْأَخِيرَةُ هِيَ رُوْحُ (الْإِيمَانِ) وَهِيَ مِنْحَةٌ مِنَ نُّورِ النَّبِى ?:

    3) The last spirit is the spirit of "Al-Imaan" (The Faith). And it is a giving from the light of the Prophet ?:

    أَمَّا عَنِ الإِيمَانِ فَهُوَ مُغَيَّبٌ *** فِي طَيِّ غَوْرِ الْقَلْبِ وَالْوِجْدَانِ
    As regard to the Imaan (Faith), then it is hidden…
    In the folding of the depth of the heart and the consciousness
    Qasida 6:12

    هُوَ مِنْحَةٌ مِنْ نُورِ أَحْمَدَ أَهْلُهَا *** رُزِقُوا بِهَا خَرُّوا إِلَى الأَذْقَانِ
    It is a giving from the light of Ahmad ?, and those worthy of it…
    Are fed by it, while they drop their chins
    Qasida 6:14

    وَعِنَدَمَا يَصِلُ الآدَمِىُّ إِلَى مَوْطِنِ التَّكْلِيفِ تَأْتِى (اَلْأَنْفُسُ) وَيَبْدَأُ اَلصِّرَاعُ بَينَ اَلطَّبَائِعِ اَلْأَرْبَعَةِ اَلْمُمْتَزِجَةً فِيهِ، مِنْهَا اَلرَّحْمَانِى اَلْمُتَمَثِّلُ فِي (التُّرَابِ وَالْمَاءِ) وَالشَيْطَانِى اَلْمُتَمَثِّلُ فِى (الْهَوَاءِ وَالنَّارِ) وَيَسْتَمِرُّ الصِّرَاعُ حَتَّى تَنْتَصُرَ أَحَدَ هَذِهِ الطَّبَائِعُ، وَعَنِ الْحَبِيبِ اَلْمُصْطَفَى ? أَنَّهُ قَالَ: خَلَقَ الله اَلْإِنْسَانَ مِنْ أَرْبَعَةِ أَشْيَاءٍ: مِنَ الْمَاءِ وَالطِّينِ وَالنَّارِ وَالرِّيحِ، أَمَّا إِذَا كَثُرَ مِنَ الْمَاءِ فَيَكُونَ حَافِظاً أَوْ عَالِماً فَقِيهاً أَوْ كَرِيماً، وَأَمَّا إِذَا كَثُرَ مِنَ الطِّينِ فَيَكُونَ سَفَّاكاً خَبِيثاً مُفْلِساً فِى الدُّنْيَا وَالْآخِرَةِ، وَأَمَّا إِذَا كَثُرَ مِنَ النَّارِ فَيَكُونَ عَوَّاناً أَوْ ظَالِماً، وَأَمَّا إِذَا كَثُرَ مِنَ الرِّيْحِ فَيَكُونَ كَذَّاباً. الحافظ السيوطى.

    When the human reaches the age of puberty, then comes (the ego's) and the conflict begins between the four elements of nature mixed inside him. There is one part called "The Rahmani" (The Beneficial) represented by "Dust and Water" and other one is "The Satanic" represented by "Air and Fire" and the conflict continues until one of these naturel elements prevails. The Beloved Al-Mustafa ? have said: "Allah created the human from four things: Water, Earth, Fire, and Wind. If water the exceeds, then he will be a preserver or a knowledgeable scholar or a generous one, but if the Earth exceeds, then he will be cruel, evil and broke in this world and the Hereafter. If the Fire exceeds, he will be a supporter or an unjust and if the Wind exceeds, then he will be a big liar". (Hafiz Al-Suyuti).

    ثُمَّ أَنَّ الله وَضَعَ لِاِبْنِ آدَمَ (اَلْعَقْلَ) وَهُوَ مِثَالٌ لِلْبَرْزَخِ وَهُوَ لُغَةً بِمَعْنَى (اَلرِّبَاطٌ) حَيْثُ وَرَدَ فِى الْحَدِيثِ قَولُ الصَّحَابِى لِلنَّبِى ? مُتَحَدِّثاً عَنْ نَاقَتِهُ: أَأَعْقِلُهَا وَأَتَوَكَّلُ أَمْ أَتْرُكُهَا وَأَتَوَكَّلُ؟ قَالَ: بَلْ اِعْقِلْهَا وَتَوَكَّلْ، أَىْ اِرْبِطْهَا، أَىْ أَنَّ الْعَقْلَ خُلِقَ لِرَبْطِ الْأَشْيَاءِ، فَلَا يَعْمَلُ الْآدَمِىُّ مِنْ تِلْقَاءِ نَفْسِهِ وَوِفْقَ هَوَاهُ بَلْ هُوَ مُقَيَّدٌ بِضَوَابِطٍ مُعَيَّنَةٍ، وَهِيَ الْأَوَامِرُ الْإِلَهِيَّةُ وَشِرْعَةِ النَّبِى ?، وَهِيَ مَا تُسَمَّى بِالْمَعْقُولَاتِ، وَهُوَ مَا يَتَّفِقُ مَعَ الرَّبْطِ، لِذَلِكَ فَإِنَّ الْفِقْهَ فِى أَوَّلِ دَرَجَاتِهِ، وَالْإِسْلَامَ فِى أَوَّلِ دَرَجَاتِهِ، وَالدِّينَ فِى أَوَّلِ دَرَجَاتِهِ، يَحْتَاجُ لِهَذَا الرَّبْطِ، فَتَجِدُ الْآيَاتِ تَقُولُ (أَفَلَا يَعْقِلُونَ) 68 يس، أَىْ رَبْطُ الْأَمْرِ بِتَنْفِيذِهِ،

    Then Allah has placed "Al-Aql" (The Mind) inside the son of Adam, and it is similar to "Al-Barzakh" (The Partition) which is a linguistic idiom for (The Bond). So, when the story of a companion is mentioned in the Hadith of the prophet ? were he speaks about his she-camel asking: "Should I tie it up and rely (upon Allah) or should I leave it untied while relying (upon Allah)? He ? replied: "You should tie it up and then rely upon Allah", meaning that he should bind it, which leads us understand that the mind is created to tie things up. So, the human being should not perform autonomously on his own or according to his own whims, but he is rather restricted by certain specified regulations, which are the preordained divine laws together with the regulations of the Prophet ? and this is what is called the reasonable, and this is consistent with "The bonding". Because of this the jurisprudence the first level, Islam in the first level, and the religion in first level, they all need this bonding, so you will find several Ayat sayings "So, do they not reason?" (Yasin, 36:68), which means, the bonding of the order with the execution of it.

    وَالْعَقْلُ يَسْكُنُ مَوْطِنَ الْحَواسِ، وَهُوَ مَوْطِنُ الْقَلْبِ لَا اَلرَّأْسِ، قاَلَ تَعَالَى (أَفَلَمْ يَسِيرُوا فِى الْأَرْضِ فَتَكُونَ لَهُمْ قُلُوبٌ يَعْقِلُونَ بِهَا أَوْ آذَانٌ يَسْمَعُونَ بِهَا) 46 الحج، وَهَذَا الْعَقْلُ يَرْتَقِى، فَيُصْبِحُ فُؤَاداً، فَعَلَى سَبِيلِ الْمِثَالِ فِى قِصَّةٍ سَيَّدَنَا مُوسَى وَأُمِّهِ، فَقَدْ ظَهَرَتْ نَبُوْءَةُ الْوَلَدِ مِنْ بَنِى إِسْرَائِيلَ الَّذِى يُهَدِّدُ مُلْكَ فِرْعَونَ، فَكَانَ فِرْعَونُ يَبْعَثُ بِجُنُودِهِ لِقَتْلِ كُلِّ حَامِلٍ مِنْ بَنِى إِسْرَائِيلَ، فَبَعْدَ وِلَادَتِهِ عَلَيْهِ السَّلَامِ دَخَلَ الْجُنُودُ فَجْأَةً فَرَمَتْهُ أُخْتُهُ فِى التَّنُّوْرِ وَهُوُ الفُرْنُ، وَلَمَّا خَرَجَ الْجُنُوْدُ نَظَرَتْ أُخْتُهُ فِى التَّنُّورِ فَوَجَدَتْهُ جَالِساً يَرْضَعُ إِصْبَعَهُ، وَهَذَا هُوَ الْخَارِجُ عَنِ الْمَعْقُولِ،

    And the mind resides in the locality of the senses, which is the locality of the heart and not the head. The Almighty have said, "Have they not travelled in the Earth, so they have hearts by which they can reason or ears by which they can hear" (46 Hajj). And This mind further develops and so that it becomes a "Fouad" (The Enlightened Heart). For example, in the story of Sayyidina Musa and his mother, when the prophecy about the boy from the Israelites who were to threaten the sovereignty of Pharaoh, was shown to Pharaoh, so he sent his soldiers to kill every new-born boy from the sons of Israel. After his birth (Peace be upon him), when the soldiers suddenly entered inside, his sister then threw him in the furnace, which is the oven. And when the soldiers had left, his sister looked into the oven and found him sitting while suckling on his finger. And this is beyond the reasonable.

    فَقَرَّرَتْ حِينَهَا أُمُّهُ أَنْ تُلْقِى بِهِ فِى الْيَمِّ وَ (إِنْ كَادَتْ لِتُبْدِى بِهِ لَوْلَا أَنْ رَبَطْنَا عَلَى قَلْبِهَا) (وَأَصْبَحَ فُؤَادُ أُمِّ مُوسَى فَارِغًا) 10 القصص، أَىْ إِنَّهُ اِسْتَقَرَّ، وَأَصْبَحَ فَارِغاً عَنْ كُلِّ شَئٍ سِوَى الله فَقَالَ لَهَا تَعَالَى (وَأَوْحَيْنَا إِلَى أُمِّ مُوسَى أَنْ أَرْضِعِيهِ فَإِذَا خِفْتِ عَلَيْهِ فَأَلْقِيهِ فِى الْيَمِّ وَلَا تَخَافِى وَلَا تَحْزَنِى إِنَّا رَادُّوهُ إِلَيْكِ وَجَاعِلُوهُ مِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ) 7 القصص، وَقَالَ: (أَنِ اقْذِفِيهِ فِي التَّابُوتِ فَاقْذِفِيهِ فِي الْيَمِّ فَلْيُلْقِهِ الْيَمُّ بِالسَّاحِلِ يَأْخُذْهُ عَدُوٌّ لِي وَعَدُوٌّ لَهُ) 39 طه، وَهَذَا هُوَ الْخَارِجَ عَنِ الْمَعْقُولِ وَهُوَ مَا يَخْتَصُّ بِهِ الْفُؤَادَ وَيَتَقَبَّلَهُ، ثُمَّ بَعْدَ الْفُؤَادِ يَتَطَوَّرُ الْإِيْمَانُ إِلَى الْلُبِّ وَهُوَ عَقْلُ الرُّوحِ، فِى قَوْلِهِ تَعَالَى (وَلِيَتَذَكَّرَ أُولُو الْأَلْبَابِ) 29 ص، ثُمَّ يَتَرَقَّى لِيُصْبَحَ عَقْلُ الْعَقْلِ، وَهُوَ الْعَقْلُ الذَّاتِي، وَهُوَ مَا يَتَفَكَّرُ فِى اللهِ وَكُلُّ مَا دُونَهُ يَتَفَكَّرَ فِى آيَاتِ اللهِ، وَصَاحِبُ الْعَقْلِ الذَّاتِي يُسَمَّى بِ (ذِى النُّهَى):

    Then his mother decided to throw him in the river and "She was about to reveal him, had We not bound fast her heart" (Al-Qasas, 28:10). "And the heart of Moses' mother became empty" (Al-Qasas, 28:10). This Means that it was, he independent and became empty from everything except Allah. So, The Lord Almighty said to her, "And We inspired to the mother of Moses, "Suckle him; but when you fear for him, cast him into the river and do not fear and do not grieve. Indeed, we will return him to you and will make him among the Messengers." (Al-Qasas, 28:7), and He further said, "Cast him into the chest and cast it into the river, and the river will throw him onto the riverbank; there an enemy to Me and an enemy to him, will take him." (Taha, 20:39), and this is also beyond the reasonable. So, this is the distinctive feature of the "Fouad" (The Enlightened Heart), and can only be accepted by. Then after being the "Fouad ", the "Imaan" (Faith) develops even further towards becoming "Al-Lub" (The Divine Understanding) and is the mind of the soul, as in the saying of the Almighty: "So that those of understanding would be reminded." (Sad, 38:29). Then it develops further and becomes the mind of the mind, which is mind of the self. And this is the mind which think in reflect upon Allah while everything else beneath it reflects upon the signs of Allah. The possessor of the mind of the self is called "Possessor of the Nuhaa" (The Independent Intelligence):

    أَتَحْسَبُ أَنَّكَ جِرْمٌ صَغِيرٌ *** وَفِيْكَ انْطَوَى اَلْعَالَمُ الْأَكْبَرُ
    Do you think that you are, just a small body? …
    While the biggest universe evolves inside of you

    فَاِبْنُ آدَمَ هُوَ مِثَالٌ مُصَغَّرٌ لِلْأَكْوَانِ.
    So, the son of Adam is a miniature example of the universes.

    أَنْ يَكُوْنَ لَكَ عَقْلٌ هَذَا أَمْرٌ، وَأَنْ يَكُوْنَ هَذَا الْعَقْلُ مَرْبُوطاً بِنُّورِ الْفَهْمِ هَذَا أَمْرٌ آخِرٌ .. لِأَنَّ الْجَمِيعَ لَهُمْ عَقْلٌ وَلَكِنْ لَيسَ لِكُلٍّ نُورَ الْفَهْمِ، وَنُورُ الْفَهْمِ لَا يَتَأَتَّى إِلَّا بِمُدَاوَمَةِ الذِّكْرِ، وَالْفِكْرُ يَأْتِى بَعْدَ الذِّكْرِ.
    That you have a mind is one thing, but to have this mind tied to the light of understanding is another thing. Because everyone has a mind, but not everyone has the light of understanding and the light of understanding does never comes except by constantly being in dhikr, and the reflection comes after the dhikr.

    نِلْنَا مُنَانَا وَمَا فَوْقَ الْمُنَى دُرَراً
    We have obtained our desired objective…
    and that which is above the desired objective, as an abundance of pearls
    (Qasida 28:13)

    مَازِلْنَا نَنْهَلُ مِنْ نَبْعِ هِدَايَةِ أَقْوَامٍ أَفْنَوا أَعْمَارَهُمْ فِى الدَّعْوَةِ الْخَالِصَةِ إِلَى اللهِ وَهَا هُوَ اَلْإِمَامُ الشَافِعِى RA يَقُولُ: طَلَبْنَا تَرْكَ الذَّنُوبِ فَوَجَدْنَاهَا فِى صَلَاةِ الضُّحَى، وَطَلَبْنَا ضِيَاءَ الْقُبُورِ فَوَجَدْنَاهَا فِى قِرَاءَةِ الْقُرآنِ، وَطَلَبْنَا عُبُورَ الصِّرَاطِ فَوَجَدْنَاهَا فِى الصَّومِ وَالصَّدَقَةِ، وَطَلَبْنَا ظِلَّ الْعَرْشِ فَوَجْدَنَاهَا فِى الْأُخُوَّةِ الصَّالِحَةِ، أَفَاضَ اللهُ عَلَى قُلُوبِكُمْ نُورَ الرِضْوَانِ وَأَسْمَاعِكُمْ عُذُوبَةَ الْقُرآنِ.

    We are still drinking from the spring of the guidance belonging to the nations whom had spent their lifetimes in purest calling to Allah, and here Imam Shafi'i (ra) says: "We searched for the leaving of the sins and We discovered it in the prayer of "Ad-Duhaa" (Morning-Light), We searched for the illumination of the graves and We discovered it the reading of Qur'an, and We searched for crossing of "As-Sirat" and We discovered it the fasting and almsgiving and We searched for the Shade of the Throne and we discovered it in the devoted brotherhood". May Allah overflow your hearts by the light of satisfaction and your hearing by the sweetness of Qur'an.

    فَلَا تَكُنْ بِمَا نِلْتَ مِنْ دُنْيَاكَ فَرِحاً، وَلَا لِمَا فَاتَكَ مِنْهَا تَرِحاً وَلَا تَكُنْ مِمَّنْ يَرْجُو الْآخِرَةِ بِغَيْرِ عَمَلٍ، وَيُؤَخِّرُ التَّوبَةَ لِطُولِ أَمَلٍ، فَمَنْ شَغَلَتْهُ دُنْيَاهُ خَسِرَ آخِرَتَهُ.

    Don't be exulted, with what you have achieved from your life and don't grieve, upon what has eluded you and don't be, from those expecting success in the afterlife without working for it, while delaying the repentance for tomorrow, out of hope, since whoever was kept busy by his life, had lost his afterlife.

    وَمَهْمَا كَانَتْ ظُرُوفُكَ الْقَاحِلَةَ سَيُنْبِتُ اللهُ لَكَ فِيهَا الْفَرَجُ، فَتَأَمَّلْ قَوْلُهُ تَعَالَى فِى كِتَابِهِ الْكَرِيمِ (وَارْزُقْهُمْ مِنَ الثَّمَرَاتِ) 37 إبراهيم، فَكَانَتْ تِلْكَ الدَّعْوَةَ رَغْمَ أَنَّهُمْ بِوَادٍ غَيرِ ذِى زَرْعٍ.

    And however arid (infertile) your circumstances should be, then Allah is going to produce a happy outcome from this for you, so you should contemplate upon His saying in the Noble Book: "And Provide for them from the benefits" (Ibrahim 14:37), So, that supplication was despite that they were in an uncultivated valley.

    وَعَنِ الثَّبَاتِ فِى الْأَمْرِ فَإِنَّهُ لَا يَكُونَ بِكَثْرَةِ الْاِسْتَمَاعِ لِلْمَوَاعِظِ وَإِنَّمَا يَكُونُ (بِفَعْلِ) هَذِهِ الْمَوَاعِظِ وَتَطْبِيقِهَا فِى وَاقِعِ الْحَيَاةِ ... فَانْظُرْ إِلَى قَوْلِهُ تَعَالَى (وَلَوْ أَنَّهُمْ فَعَلُوا مَا يُوعَظُونَ بِهِ لَكَانَ خَيْرًا لَهُمْ وَأَشَدَّ تَثْبِيتًا) 66 النساء.

    As for the strengthening in the affairs, it is not so much about listening to the sermons but to be (applying) these sermons and the implementation of them in the real life ... So, consider the saying: "But if they had done what they were advised by, it would have been better for them and more intense strengthening" (An-Nisa, 4:66).

    وَقِيلَ لْأَحَدِ الْعُلَمَاءِ: يَا شَيْخْ، فَلَانٌ انْتَكَسَ.
    One scholar was told: O Shaykh, that one has fallen back (to his previous condition of heedlessness).

    قاَلَ الشَيْخُ: لَعَلَّ انْتِكَاسَتِهِ مِنْ أَمْرَينِ:
    The Shaykh replied: Perhaps relapse is a result of two things:

    اَلْأَوَّلُ: إِنَّمَا أَنَّهُ لَمْ يَسْأَلْ اللهَ الثَّبَاتَ، أَوْ الثَّانِى: أَنَّهُ لَمْ يَشْكُرِ اللهَ عَلَى نِعْمَةِ الْاِسْتِقَامَةِ.

    Firstly: Perhaps he didn't ask Allah for the strengthening, or the secondly: Perhaps he didn't thank Allah for the blessing of righteousness.

    فَحِينَ اِخْتَارَكَ اللهُ لِطَرِيقِ هِدَايَتِهِ، لَيْسَ لِأَنَّكَ مُمَيَّزٌ أَوْ لِطَاعَةٍ مِنْكَ، بَلْ هِىَ رَحْمَةٌ مِنْهُ شَمِلَتْكَ، قَدْ يَنْزِعُهَا مِنْكَ فِى أَىْ لَحْظَةِ، لِذَلِكَ لَا يَغْتَرُّ أَحَدٌ بِعَمَلِهِ وَلَا بِعِبَادِتِهِ، وَلَا يَنْظُرُ بِاسْتِصْغَارٍ لِمَنْ ضَلَّ عَنْ سَبِيلِهِ، فَلَوْلَا رَحْمَةُ اللهِ بِكَ لَكُنْتَ مَكَانَهُ.

    So, When Allah have chosen you for the path of his guidance, then is not because you are special or because of your obedience, but rather it is a mercy from him that includes you. He may take it away from you at any moment, so no one should be deceived by his deeds or his worship, and he should not look with denigration upon the people who have strayed, because had it not been for the mercy of Allah upon you, then you would be the one, in his place.

    قَالَ تَعَالَى (وَلَوْلَا أَنْ ثَبَّتْنَاكَ لَقَدْ كِدْتَ تَرْكَنُ إِلَيْهِمْ شَيْئًا قَلِيلًا) 74 الإسراء، فَإِيَّاكَ أَنْ تَظُنَّ أَنَّ الثَّبَاتَ عَلَى الْاِسْتَقَامَةِ أَحَدُ إِنْجَازَاتِكَ وَإِنَّمَا تَوْفِيقُ مِنَ اللهِ، نَسْأَلُ اللهَ تَعَالَى لَنَا وَلَكُمُ الثَّبَاتَ حَتَّى الْمَمَاتِ، وَقَالُوا خَمْسُ مُثَبِّتَاتٍ فِى عَصْرِ الْفِتَنِ:

    He said, "And if We had not strengthened you, you would almost have inclined a little something towards them." 74 al-Isra. So, O' you, never assume! that the strengthening upon righteousness is one of your own achievements, but rather it is a consent from Allah. We ask Allah to strengthen us and strengthen you until the death, and they have given five strengthening factors in the age of temptation:

    1. الْقُرْآنُ الْكَرِيمِ (كَذَلِكَ لِنُثَبِّتَ بِهِ فُؤَادَكَ) 32 الفرقان.

    1. The Noble Quran, "Thus, that We may strengthen thereby your heart." (Al-Furqan, 25:32).

    2. وَقَرَاْءَةُ السِيرَةُ وَقَصَصُ الْأَنْبِيَاءِ (وَكُلًّا نَقُصُّ عَلَيْكَ مِنْ أَنْبَاءِ الرُّسُلِ مَا نُثَبِّتُ بِهِ فُؤَادَكَ) 120 هود.

    2. Reading the biography of the Prophet ? and the stories of the prophets "And each story, We relate to you from the news of the messengers is that by which We strengthen your heart." (Hud, 11:120).

    3. الْعَمَلُ بِالْعِلْمِ (وَلَوْ أَنَّهُمْ فَعَلُوْا مَا يُوعَظُونَ بِهِ لَكَانَ خَيْرًا لَهُم وَأَشَدَّ تَثْبِيتًا) 66 النساء.

    3. Acting upon knowledge, "But if they had done what they were advised, it would have been better for them and a more intense strengthening" (An-Nisa, 4:66).

    4. الْدُّعَاءُ (يَا مُقَلِّبَ الْقُلُوبِ ثَبِّتْ قَلْبِيَّ عَلَى طَاعَتِكَ (

    4. The Supplication, "O' the Turner of the Hearts! Strengthen my heart upon your obedience"

    5. الْرِفْقَةُ الْصَالِحَةُ (وَاصْبِرْ نَفْسَكَ مَعَ الَّذِينَ يَدْعُونَ رَبَّهُمْ بِالْغَدَاةِ وَالْعَشِى يُرِيدُونَ وَجْهَهُ .. ) 28 الكهف

    5. Good companionship, "And keep yourself patient [by being] with those who call upon their Lord in the morning and the evening, seeking His countenance." (Al-Kahf, 18:28).

    وَمِنْ وَصَايَا أَحَدِ الْعَارِفِينَ لِكُلِّ الْمُرِيدِينَ وَالسَّالِكِينَ فِى طَّرِيقِ الْقَوْمِ، فِي طَّرِيقِ التَّصَوُّفِ وَخَاصَةَ الْفُقَرَاءِ، وَهِيَ وَصِيَّةٌ عَظِيْمَةٌ تُعَدُّ بِمَثَابَةِ الْعَقِيدَةِ وَالدُّسْتُورِ وَالْمَنْهَجِ لِكُلِّ سَالِكٍ يُرِدْ طَرِيقَ أَهْلِ اللهِ، فَيَنْبَغِى أَنْ تَكُوْنَ مَحْفُوْظَةً لَدَى كُلِّ مُرِيدٍ فِى عَقْلِهِ وَقَلْبِهِ، فَهِىَ كَالْمَشْعَلِ يُضِئُ لَكَ الْطَرِيقَ فِى ظُلْمَةِ الْلَيْلِ الْحَالِكِ فَاحْفَظْهَا وَاعْمَلْ بِهَا وَهَذِهِ هِىَ الْوَصِيَّةُ:

    And from the commandments of one of the Gnostics (Sayyidina Abdul Qadir Jilani ra)to all the Murids and the travellers to Allah in the Path of the folks, in the path of Sufism and especially "Al-Fuqara" (those poor out of the constant need from Allah), is this great commandment, which is taken as creed, constitution and syllabus by every traveller, who desires the path of the people of Allah. It should be preserved by every murid in his mind and his heart, as it is like a torch, which illuminates the path for you in deep darkness of the night. So, save it! and work by it! And this is the commandment:

    أُوصِيكَهَا نُوحِيَّةً فَاعْمَلْ بِهَا *** فِي ذِكْرِهَا غَصَّتْ صِحَافُ الأَوَّلِينْ

    1. أُوْصِيكَ بِتَقْوَى اللهِ، وَحِفْظُ طَاعَتِهِ، وَلَزُومُ ظَاهِرُ الشَّرِيعَةِ، وَحِفْظُ حُدُودِهِ.
    1. I recommend you, by the carefulness from Allah, by the preserving of His obedience, by the obligation to abide to the apparentness of the sharia and by preserving His restrictions.

    Allimu part 2

    ومراتب الدين أربعة:
    1. توفيق
    ?. تفويض:
    (3). توكل.
    4. تسليم.
    مش كده؟ توفيق، تفويض، توكل، تسليم مش كده؟ التوفيق يعنى إيه؟ إحنا بنشوف التوفيق معناه الاستعانة، لأ التوفيق معناه الصنف البشرى عنده أخلاق شيطانية شريرة وربنا بيطالبه بأخلاق نبوية {لَقَدْ كَانَ لَكُمْ فِي رَسُولِ اللَّهِ أُسْوَةٌ حَسَنَةٌ لِمَنْ كَانَ يَرْجُو اللَّهَ وَالْيَوْمَ الآخِرَ وَذَكَرَ اللَّهَ كَثِيرًا} (183) [الأحزاب: 21] ربنا بيطالبنا بأخلاق النبي عليه الصلاة والسلام ده معنى التوفيق، التفويض ترك التدبير والقبول بتدبير ربنا ليك ده التفويض، التوكل مريد بيصل مرتبة يشوف كل ما في الوجود بيشغله عن الله بيتكي ظهره على الله (من توكل على الله كفاه)، {مَنْ يَتَوَكَّلْ عَلَى اللَّهِ فَهُوَ حَسْبُهُ} (183) [الطلاق: 3] التسليم خد ده مش استلمته؟ فسيدنا عبد الله بن عمر كان في موطن التسليم، هو والنبي عليه الصلاة والسلام ماشيين بيقول ليه ألا أوصيك يا غلام؟ قال له: نعم، قال له: إذا توكلت فتوكل على الله، و (إذا) حرف لما يستقبل من الزمان، يعني إذا نزلت من التسليم التحت جيت في التوكل تتوكل على الله، مين فيكم ماشي للتسليم وجاي للتوكل، فالكلام ده ما ليكم، والوهابية غلطانين.
    مقامات التقوى أربعة: التوفيق والتفويض والتوكُّل والتسليم
    The levels of "At-Taqwa" are four: The Accordance, The Delegation, The Reliance, The Submission

    2. وَإِنَّ طَرِيقَتَنَا هَذِهِ مَبْنِيَّةٌ عَلَى: سَلَامَةِ الصَّدْرِ، وَسَمَاحَةِ النَّفْسِ، وَبَشَاشَةِ الْوَجْهِ، وَبَذْلِ النَّدَى، وَكَفِّ الْأَذَى، وَالصَّفْحِ عَنْ عَثْرَاتِ الْإِخْوَانِ.
    2. Our Tariqa (Path) is based upon: the soundness of the chest, the large-heartedness of the self, the cheerfulness of the face, the diligence of the generosity, the abstaining from the harming and overlooking of the mistakes of the Brothers.
    3. وَأُوْصِيكَ بِالْفَقْرِ هُوَ: حِفْظُ حُرُمَاتِ الْمَشَايْخِ، وَحُسْنُ الْعِشْرَةِ مَعَ الْإِخْوَانِ، وَالنَّصِيحَةُ لِلْأَصَاغِرِ، وَالشَّفَقَةُ عَلَى الْأَكَابِرِ، وَتَرْكُ الْخُصُومَةِ مَعَ النَّاسِ، وَمُلَازَمَةُ الْإِيْثَارِ، وَمُجَانَبَةُ الْاِدِّخَارِ وَتَرْكُ الصُّحْبَةِ مَعَ مَنْ لَيْسَ مِنْهُمْ وَمِنْ طَبَقَتِهِمْ، وَالْمُعَاوَنَةُ فِي أَمْرِ الدِّينِ وَالدُّنْيَا وَحَقِيقَةُ الْفَقْرِ أَنْ لَا تَفْتَقِرَ إِلَى مَنْ هُوَ مِثْلُكَ وَحَقِيقَةُ الْغِنَى أَنْ تَسْتَغْنِى عَمَّنْ هُوَ مِثْلُكَ.
    3. And I recommend you to the poverty which is: to preserve the sanctity of the sheikhs, to improve the companionship with the Brothers, to advise the younger ones, to be affectionate towards the older ones, to leave the disputation with the people, to adhere to altruism, and to avoid the hoarding, and to abandon the company of those who are not from among them and their group, and to aid in the matters of the religion and the worldly life. And the reality of the poverty is, that you are not dependent upon one similar to you. And the reality of the richness is to be dispensable of one similar to you.
    4. وَأَنَّ الْتَّصَوُّفَ مَا هُوَ مَأْخُوذٌ عَنِ الْقِيلَ وَالقَالَ، بَلْ هُوَ مَأْخُوذٌ مِنْ تَرْكِ الدُّنْيَا وَأَهْلِهَا، وَقَطْعُ الْمَأْلُوفَاتِ وَالْمُسْتَحَبَّاتِ، وَمُخَالَفَةُ النَّفْسِ وَالْهَوَى، وَتَرْكُ الْاِخْتِيَارَاتِ وَالْإِرَادَاتِ وَالشَّهَوَاتِ، وَمُقَاسَاتُ الْجُوعِ وَالسَّهَرِ، وَمُلَازَمَةُ الْخَلْوَةِ وَالْعُزْلَةِ أَىْ الْمُرَاقَبَةِ.
    4. " Tassawuf" (Sufism, purification) is not obtained through empty talk, but rather it is obtained through abandonment of the worldly life and its inhabitants, through disconnecting from the usual routines and the desirable, through opposing the egos and the whims, through abandonment of the choosing, the wanting and the lusts, and through experiencing the hunger and the staying awake, and through persistence in isolation and seclusion, which means the "Muraqba" (The Observation).

    5. وَأُوْصِيكَ إِذَا رَأَيْتَ الْفَقِيرَ أَنْ لَا تَبْتَدِئَهُ بِالْعِلْمِ بَلِ ابْتَدِئَهُ بِالْحِلْمِ وَالرِّفْقِ فَإِنَّ الْعِلْمَ يُوْحِشُهُ وَالرِّفْقَ يُؤْنِسُهُ.
    5. I recommend you, that if you see the poor to not initiate by the knowledge but initiate by the leniency and gentleness, because the knowledge will desolate him while the gentleness will rejoice.

    6. وَأَنَّ التَّصَوَّفَ مَبْنِى عَلَى عِدَّةِ خِصَالٍ:

    6. And the Sufism is based upon several qualities:

    · الْأُولَى: الْسَّخَاءُ وَهِيَ لِسَيَّدِنَا إِبْرَاهِيمَ as

    First: The Generosity, which is for our Master Abraham as
    · الْثَانِيَةُ: الْرِضَا وَهِيَ لِسَيِّدِنَا إِسْحَاقَ as

    Second: The Satisfaction, which is for our Master Isaac as

    · الْثَالِثَةُ: الْصَبْرُ وَهِيَ لِسَيِّدَنَا أَيُّوْبَ as

    Third: The Patience, which is for our Master Job as

    · الْرَابِعَةُ: الْإِشَارَةُ وَهِيَ لِسَيِّدَنَا زَكَرِيَّا as

    Fourth: The language of signs, which is for our Master Zakaria as

    · الْخَامِسَةُ: الْغُرْبَةُ وَهِيَ لِسَيِّدَنَا يَحْيَى as

    Fifth: The Uniqueness, which is for our Master Yahya as

    · الْسَادِسَةُ: الْفَقْرُ وَهِيَ لِسَيِّدَنَا مُحَمَّدٌ ? (وَهِيَ الْاِفْتِقَارُ إِلَى اللهِ).

    Sixth: The Poverty, which is for Our Master Muhammad ? (And it is a need from Allah).

    7. وَأُوصِيكَ أَنْ لَا تَصْحَبْ الْأَغْنِيَاءَ إِلَّا لِلتَّعَزُّزِ، وَالْفُقَرَاءَ إِلَّا لِلتَّذَلُّلِ، وَعَلَيكَ بِالْإِخْلَاصِ وَهُوَ نِسْيَانُ رُؤْيَةِ الْخَلْقِ وَدَوَامُ رُؤْيَةِ الْخَالِقِ، وَلَا تَتَّهِمْ اللهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فِى الْأُمُورِ، وَاسْكُنْ إِلَيهِ فِى كُلِّ حَالٍ، وَلَا تُضَيَّعْ حَقُوْقَ أَخِيكَ اتِّكَالاً لِمَا بَيْنَكَ وَبَيْنَهُ مِنَ الْمَوَدَّةِ وَالصَّدَاقَةِ فَإِنَّ اللهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فَرَضَ لِكُلِّ مُؤْمِنٍ حَقُوقاً عَلَيْكَ، فَأَقَلَّ الْحَالَ هُنَا الدُّعَاءَ لَهُمْ وَخِدْمَةُ الْفُقَرَاءِ لَازِمَةٌ عَلَى طَالِبٍ بِالنَّفْسِ وَالْمَالِ.

    7. I recommend you not to accompany, the self-sufficient except for sake of (learning) the honour, and the needy except for sake of (learning) the humility. The sincerity is an obligation upon you, which is to forget viewing of the creation while constantly viewing the creator. And do not blame Allah Almighty in the matters, and be content towards Him in any condition. And do not renounce the rights of your brother taking advantage of the love and the friendship between you and him, because Allah Almighty has imposed upon you, the rights for each believer. Least of the rights being a supplication for them under all circumstances. And the serving of the poor by oneself and by the money is an obligation upon any seeker of anything.

    8. وَالْزَمْ نَفْسَكَ بِثَلَاثَةِ أَشْيَاءٍ: بِالتَّوَاضُعِ ???ِ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى وَبِحُسْنِ الْأَدَبِ مَعَ الْخَلْقِ كُلِّهِمْ وَبِسَخَاءِ النَّفْسِ.

    8. Commit yourself to three things: The modesty for the sake of Allah Almighty, good behaviour towards all the creation and the generosity in yourself.

    9. وَأَمِتْ نَفْسَكَ حَتَّى تَحْيَا، وَإِنَّ أَقْرَبَ الْخَلْقِ إِلَى اللهِ أَوْسَعَهُمْ صَدْراً وَأَحْسَنَهُمْ خُلُقاً، وَإِنَّ أَفْضَلَ الْأَعْمَالِ مُخَالَفَةُ النَّفْسِ وَالْهَوَى وَدَوَامُ التَّوَجُّهُ إِلَى اللهِ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى وَالْإِعْرَاضُ عَمَّنْ سِوَاهُ.
    9. Keep killing your egos until you become alive, and the closest among the creation to Allah is the one with the broadest chest (most tolerant, patient, generous, brave, loving) and with the best of manners. And the most superior of the deeds is the opposing of the egos and whims and constant orientation towards Allah Almighty while and leaving everything except for Him.

    10. وَحَسْبُكَ مِنَ الدُّنْيَا شَيْئَانِ: أَوَّلاً صُحْبَةُ فَقِيرٍ عَارِفٍ، ثَانِياً خِدْمَةُ وَلِيٍّ كَامِلٍ.
    10. Two thing are sufficient for you in the worldly life: One being the company of the poor gnostic, and second being in the service of a complete saint.

    11 . وَاعْلَمْ أَنَّ الْفَقِيرَ هُوَ الَّذِي لَا يَسْتَفْتِى بِشَئٍ مِنْ دُونِ اللهِ تَعَالَى، وَطَرِيقُهُ جَدٌّ كُلُّهُ فَلَا يُخَالِطَهُ بِشَئٍ مِنَ الْهَزْلِ.
    11. You should know that the poor is the one, who doesn't appeal to anyone except to Allah Almighty, and he is completely serious in his Path, so he never mixes it with anything of joking.

    12. وَجَانِبْ أَهْلَ الْبِدَعِ -وَالْعِيَاذُ بِا???ِ- فَلَا تَنْظُرْ إِلَيْهِمْ جُمْلَةً.
    12. Turn aside from the people of the innovation in the religion - May Allah protect us from them - So, don't look at them, even for a moment.

    13 . وَعَلَيْكَ بِتَرْكِ الْاِخْتِيَارِ وَمُلَازَمَةُ الْتَسْلِيمِ وَتَفْوِيضُ الْأَمْرِ كُلِّهِ ???ِ.
    13. It is an obligation upon you, to leave the choice, and adhere to the submission, and delegate of all the matters to Allah.

    وَطَيِّبُ الْصُّحْبَةِ هُمْ مَنْ إِذَا غِبْتَ عَنْهُمْ فَقَدُوكَ، وَإِذَا غَفَلْتَ نَبَّهُوْكَ، وَإِذَا دَعَوْا لِأَنْفُسِهِمْ لَمْ يَنْسَوْكَ، هُمْ كَالْنَّجُومِ إِذَا ضَلَّتْ سَفَيْنَتُكَ فِى بَحْرِ الْحَيَاةِ أَرْشَدُوكَ، وَغَداً تَحْتَ عَرْشِ الرَّحْمَنِ يَنْتَظِرُوكَ، أَلَا يَكْفِي أَنَّهُمْ فِى اللهِ أَحَبُّوكَ.
    And the good company are those, if you are absent from them, they will miss you, and if you are heedless, they will warn you, and if they supplicate for themselves, they never forget you. They are like the stars which guides your ship, when it is lost in the Sea of life and tomorrow, they will be awaiting you under the throne of the Merciful. Is it not enough that they love you for the sake of Allah?

    مِمَّا تَعَلَّمْنَاهُ مِنْ سُوَرِ الْقُرآنِ الْكَرِيمِ سُوْرَةُ "الْتَفَاؤُلُ وَالْتَثْبِيتُ" الْكَثِيرِينَ مِنَّا لَا يَعْلَمُوْنَهَا، إِنَّهَا سُوْرَةُ الْطَّلَاقِ، وَهَلْ تَعْلَمُ لِمَاذَا سُمِّيَتْ كَذَلِكَ؟
    From what we have learned from the chapters of the Noble Holy Quran, there is a chapter called "The Optimism and The Strengthening". Most of us do not know it. It is the chapter of "At-Talaq", and do you know why it is called this?

    لِأَنَّهَا حُشِدَتْ بِعَدَدٍ مِنَ الْجُمَلِ الَّتِى تَفْتَحُ لِكُلِّ مَغْمُوْمٍ وَمَهْمُومٍ آفَاقَ الْأَمَلِ عَلَى الْرَّغْمِ مِنْ قِصَرِهَا.
    Because it has collected together a number of sentences, which opens the prospects of hope for everyone sorrowful and everyone worried, despite being brief.

    إِذَا مَرَّتْ حَيَاتُنَا بِزَوَاجٍ فَاشَلٍ أَوْ مَرَضٍ أَوْ صِرَاعٍ نَفْسِي أَوْ خِسَارَةٍ مَالِيَّةٍ، فَاعْرِضْ حَالَكَ عَلَى آيَاتِ سُّورَةِ "الْتَفَاؤُلِ وَالْتَثْبِيتِ"
    If our lives have undergone a failed marriage, a sickness, a psychological trauma or a financial loss, then present you case to the verses of the chapter of "The Optimism and The Strengthening"

    بِدْءاً بِالْآيَةِ الْأُوْلَى (لَا تَدْرِى لَعَلَّ ا???َ يُحْدِثُ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ أَمْرًا) 1 الطلاق، اِجْعَلْهَا شَعَارَكَ، وَتَذَكَّرَهَا كُلَّمَا بُليْتَ بِمَا يَغُمُّكَ.
    Starting with the first verse "You don't know, perhaps Allah will bring about after that a different situation." (Talaq, 65:1). Make it your motto, and remember it whenever you are put through a trial by which you are anguished or sad.
    ثُمَّ الْآيَاتُ الَّتِى بَعْدَهَا، (وَمَنْ يَتَّقِ ا???َ يَجْعَلْ لَهُ مَخْرَجًا) 2 الطلاق
    Then the Ayat which comes after it is: "And whoever is watchful towards Allah - He makes for him a way out" (At-Talaq, 65:2).
    أَضَاقَتْ عَلَيْكَ الْدُّنْيَا حَتَّى كِدَّتْ تُجْزِمْ أَنَّكَ لَنْ تَسْعَدْ بِحَيَاتِكَ مَرَّةً أُخْرَى.
    Has the worldly life been tightened upon you to an extent, that you have almost become certain, that you will never will experience happiness in your life ever again?

    مَنْ جَعَلَ لَكَ مَدْخَلاً إِلَى هَذَا الْبَلَاءِ يَجْعَلْ لَكَ -يَقَيْناً- مَخْرَجاً مِنْهُ، إِنْ اتَّقَيْتَهُ (وَيَرْزُقْهُ مِنْ حَيْثُ لَا يَحْتَسِبُ) 3 الطلاق، لَيْسَ الْرِزْقُ مَالاً فَقَطْ، بَلْ شِفَاءً يُذهِبُ مَرَضَكَ، طُمَأْنِيْنَةً تُذهِبُ ضِيقَ صَدْرِكَ، شَرِيكاً صَالِحاً يُنسِيْكَ شَقَاءَكَ الْأَوَّلِ.
    Then the One who put you in this affliction in first place, is also The One who will make for you - with absolute certainty - a way out of it. If you are watchful towards Him "And He provides for him from where he does not anticipate it." (At-Talaq 65). The provision is not only money, rather it is a healing, which cures your sickness. It is a reassurance, which removes the tightness of your chest. It is an upright partner, who will make you forget your first misery.

    (وَمَنْ يَتَوَكَّلْ عَلَى ا???ِ فَهُوَ حَسْبُهُ) 3 الطلاق، أَىْ مُتَوَلِّي جَمِيعِ أَمْرِكَ، وَكَافِيكَ مَا أَهَمُّكَ، فَقَطْ تَوَكَّلْ عَلَيْهِ، وَاتْرُكْ لَهُ تَدْبِيرَ خَلَاصِكَ مِنْ هَذِهِ الْكُرْبَةِ، لَكِنْ لَا تَنْسَي (إِنَّ اللَّهَ بَالِغُ أَمْرِهِ قَدْ جَعَلَ اللَّهُ لِكُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدْرًا) 3 الطلاق، فَلَا تَسْتَعْجِلْ، وَلَا تَيْأَسْ، ومَهْمَا أَظْلَمَ الْلَيلِ فَضَوءُ الصُّبْحِ آتٍ لَا مَحالَ.
    " And whoever relies upon Allah - then He is sufficient for him." (At-Talaq 65). Meaning that he is the caretaker of all of your affairs. And He is sufficient for you regarding that, which is burdening you. So, just rely upon Him only, and leave it for Him to measure your salvation from this anguish. But don't forget "Allah will always accomplish His purpose. Allah has already set for everything an equal measure" (At-Talaq 65:3). So, don't be hasty, and don't despair, because no matter how dark the night may be, the light of the morning is definitely coming.

    (وَمَنْ يَتَّقِ اللَّهَ يَجْعَلْ لَهُ مِنْ أَمْرِهِ يُسْرًا) 4 الطلاق، يُيَسِّرْ لَك َماَ يُصَبِّرْ نَفْسَكَ، يُيَسِّرْ لَكَ مَا يُثَبِّتْ عَقْلَكَ، يُيَسِّرْ لَكَ مَا يَشْرَحْ صَدْرَكَ، يُيَسِّرْ لَكَ مَا يُنْهِى كَرْبَكَ.

    " And whoever fears Allah - He will make for him of His matter ease." (At-Talaq 65:4). He will make it easy for you to be patient, He will facilitate you to strengthen your mind, He will facilitate you to expand your chest, He will facilitate you in ending your distress.

    (وَمَنْ يَتَّقِ اللَّهَ يُكَفِّرْ عَنْهُ سَيِّئَاتِهِ وَيُعْظِمْ لَهُ أَجْرًا) 5 الطلاق، وَمَا هَذَا الْبَلَاءُ -إِنْ اِحْتَسَبْتَهُ- إِلَّا تَكْفِيرٌ لِسَيِّئَاتِكَ وَرِفْعَةٌ فِى دَرَجَاتِكَ، وَحَسْبُكَ بِهَذا مَغْنَماً وَسَطُ كُلِّ ذَلِكَ الْضِيقِ وَالْهَمِّ وَالْأَلَمِ. اِتَّقِ اللهَ، اِتَّقِ اللهَ، اِتَّقِ اللهَ.

    " And whoever fears Allah - He will remove for him his misdeeds and make increase his reward for him" (At-Talaq 65:5), if you had considered it thoroughly then this affliction is not except to remove for your evil deeds from you and raise our ranks, and this is enough for you as a gain in the midst of all that strain, distress and pain. So, beware of Allah! Beware of Allah, Beware of Allah.

    ثُمَّ تَأْتِى خَاتَمِةُ آيَاتِ الْتَّفَاؤُلِ قَاطِعَةً حَاسِمَةً (سَيَجْعَلُ اللَّهُ بَعْدَ عُسْرٍ يُسْرًا) 7 الطلاق، فَهَلْ يَبْقَى فِى نَفْسِكَ -أَيْهَا الْمَكْرُوبُ- شَئً مِنَ الْشَّكِّ وَالْتَّشَاؤُمِ بَعْدَ كُلِّ هَذَا؟!

    Then comes the conclusion of the Ayat of the joyous omen (The Optimism) decisively and conclusively when it is said "Allah will make after an adversity an ease" (At-Talaq 65:7). O You who are distressed - Do you still have in yourself anything of suspicion and pessimism left after all of this?!

    نِلْنَا مُنَانَا وَمَا فَوْقَ الْمُنَى دُرَراً
    We have obtained our desired objective…
    and that which is above the desired objective, as an abundance of pearls
    (Qasida 28:13)

    يَقُولُ الْمَوْلَى الْكَرِيمُ (وَسَارِعُوا إِلَى مَغْفِرَةٍ مِنْ رَبِّكُمْ وَجَنَّةٍ عَرْضُهَا السَّمَوَاتُ وَالْأَرْضُ أُعِدَّتْ لِلْمُتَّقِينَ) 133 آل عمران، فَالْمُبَادَرَةُ إِلَى مَغْفِرَةٍ مِنَ الْرَّبِّ وَالْتَّوْبَةِ وَالْإِخْلَاصِ وَسَائِرِ الْطَّاعَاتِ، أَمَرَنَا الْحَقُّ فِيهَا بِالْمُسَارَعَةِ، عَلَى غَيرِ الْحَالِ فِى هَذِهِ الْدُّنْيَا، فَنَجِدُ أَنَّ الْسُّرْعَةَ فِى مَنَاحىٍ شَتَّى أَمْرٌ غَيْرُ مَطْلُوبٍ وَيُحَذَّرُ مِنْهُ، كَالْسَّيْرِ فِى الْطَرِيقِ تَجِدُ مَنْ يُحَذِّرُكَ بِعَدْمِ الْسَّيْرِ بِسُّرْعَةٍ حِرْصاً عَلَى سَلَامَتِكَ وَسَلَامَةِ الْآخَرِينَ، أَمَّا إِذَا تَعَلَّقَ الْأَمْرُ بِطَاعَةٍ مِنَ الْطَّاعَاتِ، أَوْ قُرْبَةٍ مِنَ الْقُرُبَاتِ، أَوِ الْرُّجُوعِ إِلَى اللهِ بِتَوْبَةٍ خَالِصَةٍ، فَنَجِدُ الْحَقُّ طَلَبَ مِنَّا الْمُسَارَعَةَ، فَطَرِيقُ الْهِدَايَةِ لَا يَنْتَظِرُ الْمُتَرَاخِي، وَالْإِسْرَاعُ إِلَيْهِ هُوَ الْسَّبِيلُ، وَعَلَى قَدْرِ الْإِسْرَاعِ يَكُونُ الْاِجْتِهَادُ، وَمُنْتَهَى هَذَا الْاِجْتِهَادَ هُوَ الْجَنَّةَ بِإِذْنِ اللهِ وَآخِرُ هَذَا الْسَبِيلُ هُوَ نَوَالُ الْمُنَى بِلَذِيذِ الْخِطَابِ يَا عِبَادِي لَا تَخَافُوْا (إِنَّ الَّذِينَ قَالُوا رَبُّنَا اللَّهُ ثُمَّ اسْتَقَامُوا تَتَنَزَّلُ عَلَيْهِمُ الْمَلَائِكَةُ أَلَّا تَخَافُوا وَلَا تَحْزَنُوا وَأَبْشِرُوا بِالْجَنَّةِ الَّتِي كُنْتُمْ تُوعَدُونَ) 30 فصلت.

    The Generous Lord has said: "And hasten towards forgiveness from your Lord and a garden as wide as the heavens and earth, prepared for the righteous" (Al-Imran 3:133). So, take the initiative towards forgiveness from the Lord, and the repentance, and the sincerity and all other obedience. The Almighty has commanded us to hasten in doing so, in opposite to the case regarding the worldly life. Where we find that the hastening in various cases is not required and there is a warning against doing so. Just like driving on the road, when you find a warning sign not to drive quickly in order to ensure your safety and the safety of the others. But when it comes to the case of any act of obedience or act of proximity, or the returning to Allah through sincere repentance, we discover that the Lord Almighty demands from us, that we hasten in doing so, because the path of guidance does not await the lazy one. And hastening towards Him is the right path, and the hastening must be done with diligence, and the final result of this diligence is the Paradise by the permission of Allah and the end of this path is the granting of the desired objective by the delightful divine speech "O my servant, don't be afraid": "Indeed, those who have said, "Our Lord is Allah " and then remained constant on it - the angels will descend upon them, [saying], "Do not be afraid and do not grieve but receive good tidings of the Paradise, which you were promised" (Fussilat 41:30).

    مَا سَمِعْنَا مِثْلَ هَذَا يُومَ كَانَتْ *** يَا عِبَادِي لاَ تَخَافُوا ذَا أَمَانِي
    What we had heard was similar to this, on the day when was said…
    "O my servant, don't be afraid, this is My safe haven"
    Qasida 75:9

    وَنَحْمَدُ اللهَ إِذْ هَدَانَا بِغُرَبَاءِ الْشَّرْقِ، مَن بِهِمْ اِسْتَبَانَ الْحَقُّ، فَكَانُوا هُدَاةً مَهْدِيِيْنَ وَأَئِمَّةً رَاشِدِينَ (وَجَعَلْنَاهُمْ أَئِمَّةً يَهْدُونَ بِأَمْرِنَا وَأَوْحَيْنَا إِلَيْهِمْ فِعْلَ الْخَيْرَاتِ وَإِقَامَ الصَّلَاةِ وَإِيتَاءَ الزَّكَاةِ وَكَانُوا لَنَا عَابِدِينَ) 73 الأنبياء، مِنْهُمْ سَخِيُّ الْكَفِّ .. حَبِيبُ اللهِ .. غِيَّاثُ الْمُعَنَّى .. صَاحِبُ الْرَّحِيقِ الْمَخْتُومِ .. عَذْبُ الْشَرَابِ الَّذِي لَمْ تَشْهَدِ الْدُّنْيَا مِثَالَهُ، شَيْخُنَا وَإِمَامُنَا الْإِمَامُ فَخْرُ الْدِّينِ رَضِىَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ الَّذِي تَحَدَّثَ عَنْ نَفْسِهِ قَائِلًا:

    We praise Allah as He guided us by the strangers from the east, by whom the truth has been identified. So, they were The Guided Guides and The Righteous Leaders: "And We made them leaders guiding by Our command. And We inspired to them the doing of good deeds, establishment of the prayer, and giving of the Zakah; and they were servants only to Us." (Al-Anbya 21:73). Among them are those Generous by Hand ... Beloved by Allah ... The Saviours of those suffering. The owner of The Sealed Nectar. The owner of The Sweet drink, which the world has never witnessed the likes of him. Our Shaykh and Our Leader, Imam Fakrudin, may Allah be satisfied with him, the one who speaks of himself saying:

    عَلَى اللَّهِ الْكَرِيمِ يَكُونُ قَصْدِي *** رَضِيتُ اللَّهَ وَالإِسْلاَمَ دِينَا
    It is upon Allah, The Generous to Direct me
    So, I am satisfied with Allah (As Lord) and Islam as religion
    Qasida 57:1

    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:
    وَمِمَّا أَفَاءَ اللَّهُ ذَلِكَ مَشْرَبِي *** وَهِدَايَتِي لِلسَّالِكِينَ مَشَارِبُ
    And from that which Allah have rightfully given, that is my tavern
    And my guidance for the travellers are many taverns
    Qasida 27:10

    وَلِي نَظْمُ دُرٍّ وَالْجَوَاهِرُ مَنْطِقِي *** وَفِيهِنَّ لِلرَّاجِي النَّجَاةَ مَكَاسِبُ
    I have a composition of pearls while the jewels are my sayings
    And inside them is a reward in form of the salvation, for the hopeful one
    Qasida 27:11

    وَالَّذِي أَزَاحَ الْسِّتَارَ عَنْ مَوْلَاىَ كَمَالِ الدِّينِ رَضِىَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ مَنْ لَدَيْهِ عِوَضَ الْوَالِدَينِ وَالْحَبِيبُ الَّذِي لَا يَخِيْبُ رَجَاؤُهُ .. فَقَالَ:

    And the One who removed the cover from my Master Kamal al-Din (May Allah Almighty be satisfied with him), the one with whom was the substitution of the parents and the beloved, who's request is never turned down. He said:
    مِنْ كَمَالِ الْعَطَاءِ مِنْ فَيْضِ وَهْبٍ *** أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ جَاءَكُمْ إِبْرَاهِيمُ
    From the perfection of the bestowal, from the overflow of blessing…
    O people, Ibrahim has come to you
    Qasida 12:1
    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:
    وَصَرْحِيَ بِاسْمِ اللَّهِ بَوَّأْتُ رُكْنَهُ *** وَآيَةُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ رَفْعُ الْقَوَاعِدِ
    And by the name of "Allah" I established the corner of my tower…
    And the sign of Ibrahim is raising of the foundations
    Qasida 14:1
    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:
    ذَا أَمِينِي لَوْ عَلِمْتُمْ *** كَانَ مِنْ فَضْلِي سَرِيَّ
    This is my entrusted only if you had known…
    He was always a river of my favor
    Qasida 17:15

    وَيَسْتَمِرُّ عَطَاءُ الْإِمَامِ الْجَلِيلِ لِيُشِيرَ إِلَى الشَّرِيْفِ الْدُّسُوقِى، سَخِيُّ الرِّفْدِ .. وَالْبَطَلُ الَّذِي تَكَفَّلَ مِنْ عَوَائِدِ سِرِّهِ أَلَّا يَرُدُّ مُرِيْدَهُ صِفْرَ الْيَدِ .. يُحْكَى أَبَا الْعَيْنَيْنِ أَعْظَمَ مَاجِدِ .. فَيَقُولُ:

    The giving of the dignified Imam continues, by pointing toward the Noble El-Desouqi, The Generous Supporter. The champion, who guarantees the upholding of the tradition of his secret, to never return his followers empty handed. He is called "Aba Al-Aynain" (The owner of the place in the middle of two eyes of the Prophet ?) the Superior of those Glorified, while he says:
    مَنْ حَبَا شَيْخَكَ شَيْخاً وَاصِلاً *** يَا مُرِيدِي قُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدْ
    Who gave to your Shaikh, an authorized Shaikh? …
    O My Mureed, Say He is Allah, The Unparalleled
    Qasida 8:1

    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:
    أَبَا الْعَيْنَيْنِ يَا نَظْماً فَرِيداً *** لِأَهْلِ اللَّهِ جَمْعاً كَانَ وِرْدَا
    O "Aba Al-Aynain", O Composition of Uniqueness…
    For the travellers to Allah, He was a collecting place of watering
    Qasida 58:12

    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:
    إِنَّهُ دَارٌ وَدَيَّارُ الرِّضَا *** إِنَّهُ وَاللَّهِ حَلاَّلُ الْعُقَدْ
    He is a residence and a host of satisfaction…
    By Allah, He is the resolver of the complexities
    Qasida 8:10

    ثُمَّ يَرْسُوْ عَلَى مِيْنَاءِ الْسَّالِكِينَ فَيُطْرِبُ قُلُوبَ الْعَاشِقِينَ بِالْحَدِيثِ عَنْ سِبْطِ سَيِّدِ الْمُرْسَلِينَ وَمَعِينِ الشَّارِبِينَ وَإِمَامِ الْوَاصِلِينَ، أَبَا الْإِكْرَامِ أَبَا الْإِنْعَامِ عَطَاءِ اللهِ مَنْ دَانَتْ لَهُ كُلُّ الْرِّقَابِ، فَكَانَ لِكُلِّ الْسَّالِكِينَ بَابًا .. بِقَوْلِهِ:

    Then he anchors at the harbour of the travellers, so he delights the hearts of the lovers by talking about the grandson of the Master of the Messengers, and the fountain of the drinkers and the Imam of the connected, the owner of the honour and owner of the bestowals , the giving of Allah to whom all the necks bowed down, So He was the only door for all the travellers. By saying:

    بَابُ الدُّخُولِ إِلَى الْحَبِيبِ مُؤَكَّدٌ *** بَابُ الإِمَامِ وَنِعْمَ بَابُ الدَّانِي
    The door of the entry to the Beloved, is for sure…
    The door of the Imam and how wonderful is the door of the most near one ?
    Qasida 2:12
    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:

    فَالنُّورُ خَصَّةُ جَدِّهِ وَهُوَ الَّذِي *** زَيْتٌ لِمِشْكَاةٍ بِهَا مِصْبَاحُ
    So, the light is specialty of his grandfather while he is…
    an oil for a niche which has the lamp inside it
    Qasida 25:14

    وقال أيضاً:
    And He also said:

    سَلْ مَا تُرِيدُ مِنَ الْمَكَارِمِ عِنْدَهُ *** مُسْتَشْفِعاً فِيهِ بِتَاجِ الإِمَامَةِ
    Ask whatever you want from the noble qualities with him
    Seeking intercession in him by the crown of the Leadership
    Qasida 1:356

    وَتَرْقُصُ الْأَرْوَاحُ وَتَحِنُّ إِلَى أَوْكَارِهَا عِنْدَمَا تَشْهَدُ الْبُطُونُ الْخَفِيَّةُ بِالْكَلَامِ عَنْ سَيِّدِ الْبَرِيَّةِ، قُرَّةِ الْأَعْيَانِ وَمَلْجَأِ الْشُّفَعَاءِ، آيَةِ الْتَّوْحِيدِ وَصَاحِبِ السَّجْدَةِ يَوْمَ الزَّحَامِ، فَيُطْرِبُنَا بِقَوْلِهِ:
    The souls are dancing and longing towards their nests when the hidden interiors witnesses the talking about the Master of all creatures, the comfort of the eyes and the refuge of the intercessors, the sign of the unification and the owner of the Prostration on the day of the crowding, and he delights us by his saying:

    هُوَ أَحْمَدٌ فِي قَابِ قَوْسَيْنِ انْجَلَى *** وَلَهُ لِوَاءُ الْحَمْدِ مَا أَسْمَاهُ
    He is "The Most Praising", manifested inside the two arches…
    And only He has the banner of "The Praise", how far beyond it is!
    Qasida 19:10

    كَرَمٌ بِلاَ كَمٍّ وَلاَ كَيْفِيَّةٍ *** كَفٌّ كَرِيمٌ أَرْضُهُ وَسَمَاهُ
    A generosity without any limit and without any precondition…
    A generous hand, whether by bottom side or upper side
    Qasida 19:11

    هُوَ مَنْ عَلَيْهِ اللَّهُ صَلَّى قَدْرَ مَا *** لَا تَعْلَمُ الأَكْوَانُ كَمْ أَهْوَاهُ
    He is the one upon whom, Allah sends prayers in an amount…
    The universes have no idea about it, equal to how much I love him
    Qasida 19:18

    وَيُعَرِّجُ غُرَبَاءُ الشَّرْقِ لِتَسْلِيمِ رَايَةَ الْحَقِّ إِلَى
    الَّذِي يَجْمَعُ الْكُلَّ حَشْداً تَحْتَ رَايَتِهِ، حَامِلُ أَخْتَامِ الْحُجَّتَينِ
    وَخِتَاماً ...
    And the strangers of the east raise to hand over the banner of the truth to
    The one who gathers everyone under his banner as a crowd, the holder of the seals of the two proofs.
    In conclusion ...

    أُجِرْتُمْ عَنْ نَوَايَاكُمْ بِخَيْرٍ *** كُفِيتُمْ بِالْعَظِيمَةِ وَالإِمَامِ
    You were rewarded for your intentions by the good…
    Which is sufficient for you through the Great Lady and The Imam
    Qasida 49:10

    تَأْتِى لَنَا الْأَيَّامُ بِأَجْمَلِ وَأَطْيَبِ الْنَفَحَاتِ فَنَجِدُ مَعْ ذِكْرَى الْإِمَامَيْنِ الْجَلِيْلَيِنِ تَعَانُقاً جَمِيلاً بِذِكْرَى أَبْنَاءِ خَيْرِ الْخَلْقِ، فَمِنْ قَلِيلِ الْأَيَّامِ كَانَتْ ذِكْرَى سَيِّدَتُنَا السَيِّدَةُ زَيْنَبُ رَضِىَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهَا، وَفِى قَرِيبِ الْأَيَّامِ نَجِدُ ذِكْرَى الْمِيْنَاءُ الْأَعْظَمِ لِجَمِيعَ السَّالِكِينَ سَيِّدَنَا وَمَوْلَانَا الإِمَامُ الْحُسَينِ رَضِىَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ، وَبَيْنَ هَاتَيْنِ الْمُنَاسَبَتَينِ الْعَظِيْمَتَينِ حَوْلِيَّةُ الْإِمَامَيْنِ الْجَلِيلَيْنِ رَضِىَ اللهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى عَنْهُمَا.

    The days are bringing the most beautiful and purest gifts for us. And with the anniversary of the two venerable imams, we discover a beautiful embracing to commemorate the sons of The Best Creation ?. Few days ago, it was the anniversary of Saidana Zainab, May Allah be pleased with her, and in the coming days we will find the anniversary of the greatest port for all the travellers to Allah, Sayyidina Mawlana Al-Imam Al-Hussein, May Allah Almighty be pleased with him, and between these two great events is the yearly celebration of the two venerable Imams, May Allah Almighty, the blessed and pleased with them.

    فَالْمُنَى كُلِّ الْمُنَى أَبَداً فَوْقَ فَوْقَ الْفَوْقِ مُرْتَفِعٌ .. فَحَقاً قَالَ:
    So, the desired objective of all desired objectives is above the above of the above forever ascending. So, it is a truth when He said:
    نِلْنَا مُنَانَا وَمَا فَوْقَ الْمُنَى دُرَراً
    We have obtained our desired objective…
    and that which is above the desired objective, as an abundance of pearls
    (Qasida 28:13)

    عَهْدًا رَعَيْنَاهُ مِنْ سِرٍّ سَرَى فِينَا
    A Pledge that we watched over from a secret which circulated among us
    (Qasida 28:13)

    وصلِّ الله على سيدنا محمد وعلى آله وصحبه وسلم
    وكل عام وأنتم بخير
    ***
